                          8.2 urutteḻu veḷḷam

   72
   ikalperu maiyiṟ paṭaikō ḷañcār
       cūḻātu tuṇita lallatu vaṟituṭaṉ
       kāva letirār kaṟuttōr nāṭuniṉ
       muṉṟiṇai mutalvark kōmpiṉa ruṟaintu
       maṉpatai kāppa vaṟivu valiyuṟuttu         5
       naṉṟaṟi yuḷḷattuc cāṉṟō raṉṉaniṉ
       paṇpunaṉ kaṟiyār maṭamperu maiyiṉ
       tuñca luṟūum pakalpuku mālai
       nilampoṟai yorāanīr ñemaravan tīṇṭi
       uravuttirai kaṭukiya vurutteḻu veḷḷam         10
       varaiyā mātirak tiruḷcērpu parantu
       ñāyiṟu paṭṭa vakaṉṟuvaru kūṭṭat
       tañcāṟu puraiyu niṉṟoḻi loḻittuc
       ceñcuṭar nikaḻviṟ poṅkupicir nuṭakkiya
       maṭaṅkaṟ ṟīyi ṉaṉai yai         15
       ciṉaṅkeḻu kuruciṉiṉ ṉuṭaṟṟici ṉōrkkē.

   tuṟai: centuṟaip pāṭāṇpāṭṭu
   vaṇṇam: oḻukuvaṇṇam.
   tūkku: centūkku.
   peyar: urutteḻu veḷḷam.
   3-7. niṉmuṉṟiṇai.............perumaiyiṉ
   urai: niṉ muṉ tiṇai mutalvarkku-niṉ kuṭiyil niṉakku muṉṉē viḷaṅkiya
   muṉṉōrkaḷukku; ōmpiṉar uṟaintu-pātukāppā yiruntu; maṉpatai kāppa aṟivu
   valiyuṟuttum-makkaṭ kūṭṭattaip purattaṟku vēṇṭum neṟimuṟaikaḷai
   aṟivuṟuttum; naṉṟu aṟi uḷḷattu-aṟamē kāṇum uḷḷattaiyuṭaiya; cāṉṟōr
   aṉṉa- amaiccar pōṉṟa cūḻccivaṉmai paṭaitta; niṉpaṇpu-niṉatu iyalpiṉai;
   maṭam perumaiyiṉ-aṟiyāmai mika vuṭaiyarātalāl; naṉku aṟiyār-teḷiya
   aṟiyārāyiṉar niṉ pakaivar e-ṟu.
   aracarkku " uṟṟa nōy nīkki uṟāamai muṟkākkum peṟṟiyār" (kuṟaḷ.442)
   eṉṟaṟku, " mutalvarkku ōmpiṉaruṟaintu" eṉṟum, kuṭi yōmpal iṟaimāṭci
   yātaliṉ "maṉpatai kāppa" eṉṟum, ataṟku vēṇṭum neṟimuṟaikaḷai
   uṟaṟaviṭattuk kaḻaṟik kūṟutalum avarkkiyalpātalpaṟṟi, "aṟivu
   valiyuṟuttum" eṉṟum, aṟam vaḻuviyavaḻi araciyal naṉku naṭavātākaliṉ,
   ataṉaiyē tērntuṇarum avvamaiccar maṉappāṉmaiyai, "naṉṟaṟi yuḷḷattuc
   cāṉṟōr" eṉṟum, avaratu cūḻcci muṟrum ic cēramāṉpāl ceṟintirukkumāṟu
   tōṉṟa avarai uvamamāka niṟuttiyum kūṟiṉār. pakaivēntarum cūḻcci
   yaṭaiyarāyiṉum aṟiyāmai mikavuṭaiya reṉṟaṟku "maṭam perumaiyiṉ" eṉṟum
   ataṉāl niṉ paṇpum valiyum aṟintilar eṉṟaṟku "niṉ paṇpu naṉkaṟiyār"
   eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. aṟivu valiyuṟuttum cāṉṟōr, uḷḷattuc cāṉṟōr eṉa iyaiyum.
   paḻaiyavuraikārarum, " maṉpatai makkaṭ paṉmai" yeṉṟum, "aṟivu
   valiyuṟuttum cāṉṟōr, eṉak kūṭṭuka" eṉṟum "īṇṭuc cāṉṟō reṉṟatu
   mantirikaḷai" yeṉṟum kūṟuvar. maṭa meṉṉum eḻuvāy perumaiyiṉ eṉṉum
   payaṉilai koṇṭatu.
   8-16 tuñcal..... uṭaṟṟici ṉōrkkē.
   urai: tuñcal uṟūum pakal pukum mālai - ellā vuyirkaḷum oruṅku
   aḻitaṟkuriya ūḻik kālamāṉatu pukukiṉṟa poḻutil; nilam poṟai orāa -
   nilamakaḷ cumai nīṅka; nīrñemara vantu īṇṭi - nīr parantu vantu
   perukutalāl; uravuttirai kaṭukiya - paranteḻum alaikaḷ viraiyum:
   urutteḻu veḷḷam - nilat tuyirkaḷaik kōṟaṟkuc ciṉan teḻuvatu pōlum
   veḷḷam; varaiyā mātirattu - ellai varaiyaṟukkap paṭāta ticai muḻutum;
   iruḷ cērpu parantu - iruḷoṭu cērntu paravuvatāl; akaṉṟuvaru ñāyiṟu
   paṭṭa kūṭṭattu- iruḷaip pōkkutaṟkup paṉṉiraṇṭāy virintuvarum ñāyiṟukaḷ
   tōṉṟiya kūṭṭattiṉatu; ceñcuṭar nikaḻviṉ - civanta veyil
   nikaḻcciyiṉaiyum; poṅku picir nuṭaṅkiya - mikukiṉṟa piciriṉaiyuṭaiya av
   veḷḷattai vaṟṟacceyta; maṭaṅkal tīyiṉ-vaṭavait tīyiṉaiyum; ciṉam keḻu
   kurucil - ciṉam poruntiya kurucilē; am cāṟu puraiyum niṉ toḻil -
   aḻakiya viḻāviṉaippōla iṉpam ceyyum niṉṉuṭaiya toḻilai; oḻittu -
   vilakki; niṉ uṭaṟṟiciṉōrkku aṉaiyai - niṉṉaip pakaittup poruvārkku
   ottirukkiṉṟāy e-ṟu.
   kūṭṭattuc ceñcuṭar nikaḻviṉaiyum tīyiṉaiyum aṉaiyai eṉa iyaiyum.
   kurucil, niṉ uṭaṟṟiciṉōrkku maṭaṅkaṟṟīyiṉ aṉaiyai eṉa iyaittu muṭikka.
   orāa eṉpataṉai oruva veṉṟum, ñemara veṉpataṉai ñemarnteṉṟum, īṇṭi
   yeṉpataṉai īṇṭa veṉṟum paranteṉpataṉaip paravaveṉṟum tirittukkoḷka.
   paḻaiya vuraikārarum "orāa veṉṟataṉai oruva veṉat tirittu īṇṭi
   yeṉṟataṉaiyum īṇṭaveṉat tirikka" eṉṟum "veḷḷam paranteṉṟataṉai
   parakkaveṉat tirittu ataṉai nuṭaṅkiyaveṉa niṉṟa ceyyiya veṉṉum
   viṉaiyeccattōṭu muṭittu, ataṉaic cuṭar nikaḻvu eṉṉum toḻiṟpeyaroṭu
   muṭittu, veḷḷam parakkaiyālē avveḷḷattai māykka vēṇṭic cuṭar nikaḻtalai
   yuṭaittāṉa tīyeṉa vuraikka" eṉṟum kūṟutal kāṇka.
   ellā vuyirkaḷum oruṅku maṭiyum ūḻikkālattait "tuñcal uṟūum pakal”
   eṉṟār. iruḷ paṭarum muṭivukkāla mātalāl, "mālai" yeṉṟār. ittakaiyatoru
   kālam varutal, nilamakaṭkuc cumai nīkkam kuṟitteṉṟaṟku "nilam poṟai
   yorāa" eṉṟum, taṉṉil mūḻki maṟaiyāta iṭamum poruḷum illaiyeṉṉumāṟu nīr
   parattalāl, "nīrñemara vantīṇṭi" eṉṟum avveḷḷam kaṭukap paranteḻutaṟkuc
   cūṟaik kāṟṟu mōtutalāl pēralaikaḷ tōṉṟik kaṭukivaruvatu uyirkaṇmēṟ
   ciṉamkoṇṭu poṅki varuvatu pōṟaliṉ , uravuttirai kaṭukiya vurutteḻu
   veḷḷam" eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. "tuñcal ellā vuyirum paṭuta" leṉṟum, "paka
   leṉṟatu ūḻiyai" yeṉṟum "mālai yeṉṟatu ūḻi muṭiviṉai" yeṉṟum "urutteḻu
   veḷḷameṉṟatu palluyiraiyum oruṅku tāṉ kollum karuttuṭaiyatu pōlak
   kōpitteḻu veḷḷameṉṟavā" ṟeṉṟum "icciṟappāṉē itaṟku urutteḻu veḷḷa meṉṟu
   peyarāyiṟ" ṟeṉṟum paḻaiya vuraikārar kūṟukiṉṟār.
   ñāyiṟu mutaliya kōḷkaḷum ēṉai viṇmīṉkaḷum tōṉṟāvāṟu tiṇiyiruḷ
   parantuviṭutalāl ticaiyaṟiya lākāmaiyiṉ, "varaiyā mātirattu" eṉṟum,
   iruḷ paravumpōtē veḷḷantāṉum uṭaṉ parantu viṭumāṟu tōṉṟa, "iruḷ cērpu
   parantu" eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. iruḷeṉ puḻi uṭaṉikaḻccip poruṭṭāya oṭu vurupu
   vikarattāṟ ṟokkatu.
   akaṉṟuvaru ñāyiṟu paṭṭa kūṭṭattu eṉa māṟi iyaikka. ivvūḻiyiruḷaip pōkki
   nīrpperukkiṉai vaṟṟacceytaṟku oru ñāyiṟu amaiyāmai paṟṟip paṉṉiru
   ñāyiṟukaḷ palvēṟiṭaṅkaḷil kūṭṭamāyt tōṉṟip pēroḷiyum peru veppamum
   ceytalāl, "akaṉṟuvaru ñāyiṟu paṭṭa kūṭṭattuc ceñcuṭar nikaḻviṉ" eṉṟār.
   paṭutal, tōṉṟutal. akalutal, virital; "aḵki yakaṉṟa vaṟiveṉṉām" (kuṟaḷ
   175) eṉṟāṟpōla.
   poṅku picir eṉa veḷḷattaic cuṭṭik kūṟiṉār, picir vaṟṟuvatu pōla
   ivvūḻiveḷḷam vaṭavaittīyāl vaṟṟumāṟu tōṉṟa. nuṭakkiya veṉpaṉaic ceyyiya
   veṉṉum viṉaiyeccamākak koṇṭu "nikaḻviṉ" eṉpataṉōṭu muṭippar
   paḻaiyavuraikārar.
   "ñāyiṟu paṭṭa akaṉṟuvaru kūṭṭattuc ceñcuṭar nikaḻ" viṉāl, tiṇiyiruḷ
   keṭuvatupōla, cēraṉatu koṟṟa voḷiyiṉāl, pakaiyiruḷ keṭutalpaṟṟiyum
   maṭaṅkaṟ ṟīyāl urutteḻu veḷḷam vaṟṟuvatu pōla, pakaivaratu kaṭal maruḷ
   perum paṭai keṭṭaḻital paṟṟiyum "ceñcuṭar nikaḻviṉ maṭaṅkaṟ ṟīyiṉ
   aṉaiyai" eṉṟār. iṉi, "poṅku picir nuṭaṅkiya ceñcuṭar nikaḻviṉ" eṉṟa
   pāṭamē koṇṭu, urutteḻu veḷḷattiṉ poṅkukiṉṟa piciraik keṭutta ceñcuṭar
   nikaḻviṉaiyum, maṭaṅkaṟ ṟīyiṉaiyum aṉaiyai eṉpatē nēriya muṟaiyāta
   laṟika. ceñcuṭar nikaḻvu pēriruḷaip pōkkip picirp paṭalattaik keṭukka,
   maṭaṅkaṟṟī veḷḷattaic cuvaṟacceyyumeṉa vaṟika.
   ik kūṟiyavāṟṟāl, ciṉaṅkeḻu kurucil, niṉ uṭaṟṟiciṉōrkku, cāṟupuraiyum
   niṉ toḻiloḻittu, tuñcaluṟūum pakalpuku mālai, ñāyiṟupaṭṭa akaṉṟuvaru
   kūṭṭattuc ceñcuṭar nikaḻviṉaiyum, poṅku picir nuṭaṅkiya maṭaṅkaṟ
   ṟīyiṉaiyum aṉaiyai eṉa muṭik. viḻākkālam naṇiyār cēyār, ciṟiyār
   periyār, uṭaiyār illār, iḷaiyār mutiyār ellārkkum iṉpam ceytal pōlac
   cēramāṉum ellārkkum taṉ āṭciyāl iṉpam ceytaliṉ, "amcāṟu puraiyum niṉ
   toḻil" eṉṟār. iṉpañ ceyvatait tavirttup pakaivarkkut tuṉpam
   ceytalpaṟṟi, "niṉ toḻil oḻittu...aṉaiyai" yeṉṟār. oḻitteṉṉum
   viṉaiyeccam aṉaiyai yeṉṉum kuṟippuviṉai koṇṭatu.
   ākkam varuvittukkoḷka
   1-3 ikal.....nāṭu.
   urai: kaṟuttōr - niṉṉaip pakaittavar; ikal perumaiyiṉ paṭai kōḷ añcār -
   karuttil pakaimai peritāyiruttalpaṟṟip paṭai yeṭuttaṟku añcārāy; cūḻātu
   tuṇital allatu - kālam iṭam vali mutaliyavaṟṟai ārāyāmal pōrceyyat
   tuṇivatēyaṉṟi: nāṭu - tam nāṭṭai; uṭaṉ kāval vaṟitu etirār - palarāyk
   kūṭik kāttaṟkuc ciṟitum māṭṭārālarkāṇ e-ṟu.
   ikal, māṟupāṭu; iḵtu uḷḷattē mikka vaḻi, aṟivu toḻil ceyyāmaiyiṉ,
   ciṉattiṟ kiraiyāki mēl viḷaiyum payaṉum ārāyntilar eṉpār, "ikal
   perumaiyiṉ paṭaikōḷ añcār" eṉṟum, "cūḻātu tuṇita lallatu" eṉṟum
   kūṟiṉār. cūḻntavaḻit tamatu ciṟumaiyum niṉ perumaiyum iṉitu teḷintu
   viḷaiyakkaṭava tuṉpattukkum paḻikkum añcuva reṉpār, "añcā" reṉṟum
   palarāyk kūṭik kākkiṉum kāval nirampātu eṉṟaṟku "uṭaṉ kāvaletirār"
   eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. oru ciṟitu pōtum avarāl etirttu niṟka muṭiyātu eṉpatu
   "vaṟitu" eṉpataṉāl vaṟpuṟuttappaṭukiṟatu. paḻaiyavuraikārar, "ikal
   perumaiyiṉ eṉpataṟku ikalāṉatu peritākaiyāṉē" eṉṟum, "ikaleṉṉum
   eḻuvāykkup perumaiyai viṉainilaippayaṉilai yākak koḷka" eṉṟum
   "añcāreṉpatu viṉaiyeccamuṟ" ṟeṉṟum, "paṭaikōḷait tuṇitaleṉak kūṭṭuka "
   veṉṟum, "uṭaṉ kāva letirāreṉṟatu, palarum tammuṭ kūṭiyum kākkamāṭṭā
   reṉṟavā" ṟeṉṟum kūṟuvar
   itukāṟum kūṟiyatu: ciṉaṅkeḻu kuricil, niṉṉaik kaṟuttavar, ikal
   perumaiyiṉ paṭaikōḷañcārāyc cūḻātu tuṇitalallatu nāṭu uṭaṉ kāval
   etirār; maṭam perumaiyiṉ niṉ paṇpu naṉkaṟiyār; niṉ uṭaṟṟiciṉōrkku, niṉ
   toḻi loḻittu, nī tuñcaluṟūum pakal puku mālai, urutteḻu veḷḷam
   paravutalāl, akaṉṟuvaru kūṭṭattu ceñcuṭar nikaḻviṉaiyum maṭaṅkaṟ
   ṟīyiṉaiyum aṉaiyaiyākiṉṟāy eṉṟu viṉaimuṭivu ceyka. paḻaiya vuraikārar
   "niṉṉoṭu kaṟuttōr tam maṭam perumaiyāl niṉ muṉkuṭimutalvarkku aṟivu
   valiyuṟuttum cāṉṟōrai yotta niṉ cūḻccip paṇpuṭaimai yaṟikiṉṟilar; nītāṉ
   cūḻcciyuṭaiyaiyē yaṉṟik kurucilē, niṉṉuṭaṟṟiciṉōrkkup pōr ceyyumiṭattu
   maṭaṅkaṟ ṟīyiṉ aṉaiyai; ataṉaiyum aṟikiṉṟila rātalāl, avar tam ikal
   perumaiyāṉē añcārāyp paṭaikōḷait tuṇitalallatu nāṭṭaic ciṟitum uṭaṉkāva
   letirkoḷḷār eṉak kūṭṭi viṉaimuṭivu ceyka" eṉa muṭippar
   "itaṉāṟ colliyatu, avaṉ cūḻcciyuṭaimaiyum veṉṟic ciḻaṟappum uṭaṉ
   kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu" eṉpatu paḻaiyavurai.
   ----------------------
                     ___________________________________

                       8.3. niṟantikaḻ pāciḻai.

   73
   uravō reṇṇiṉu maṭavō reṇṇiṉum
       piṟarkku nīvāyi ṉallatu niṉakkup
       piṟaruvama mākā voruperu vēntē..........
       marutañ cāṉṟa malartalai viḷaivayaṟ
       ceyyu ṇārai yoyyu makaḷir         5
       iravum pakalum pāciḻai kaḷaiyār
       kuṟumpal yāṇark kuravai yayarum
       kāviri maṇṭiya cēyviri vaṉappiṟ
       pukāarc celva pūḻiyar meymmaṟai
       kaḻaivirin teḻutaru maḻaitavaḻ neṭuṅkōṭṭuk         10
       kollip poruna koṭittērp poṟaiyaniṉ
       vaḷaṉu māṇpaiyuṅ kaivaṇ maiyum
       mānta raḷaviṟan taṉaveṉap paṉṉāḷ
       yāṉceṉ ṟuraippavun teḷikuvar kolleṉa
       āṅkumati maruḷak kāṇkuval         15
       yāṅkuraip pēṉeṉa varuntuval yāṉē.

   ituvu matu.
   peyar: niṟantikaḻ pāciḻai.
   1-3 uravōr...vēntē.
   urai: uravōr eṇṇiṉum maḷavōr eṇṇiṉum- aṟivuṭaiyōrai yeṇṇiṉālum aḵtu
   illāta maṭavōrai yeṇṇiṉālum; piṟarkku nī uvamam vāyiṉallatu-piṟarkku nī
   uvamamāka vāyppataṉṟi;niṉakkup piṟar uvamamākā-niṉakkup piṟar uvamamā
   kalākāta; oru peru vēntē-oppaṟṟa perumaiyaiyuṭaiya vēntē e-ṟu.
   uyarntōr maṭavōr eṉṟa irutiṟattōr perumai kūṟaluṟuvārkku avariṉ
   uyarntōrai yuvamamāka vuraittalvēṇṭu meṉpatu poruḷilakkaṇa mātaliṉ,
   (tol. uvama.3) uyarvaṟa vuyarnta niṉṉaiyē avarkaṭku uvamamāka vuraittaṟ
   kamaintaṉai yeṉpār, "piṟarkku nī vāyiṉallatu niṉakkup piṟaruvamamākā
   vēntē" yeṉṟum, ākāmaikku ētu niṉatu uyarvaṟa vuyarnta perumai yeṉpār,
   "oru peru vēntē" eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. uravōr., paṭai maṭampaṭuta laṟiyāta
   aṟivuttiṇmai yuṭaiyōr; maṭavōr, koṭaikkaṇ maṭama paṭupavar; "koṭaimaṭam
   paṭutalallatu, paṭaimaṭam paṭāṉ piṟar paṭaimayak kuṟiṉē" (puṟam.142)
   eṉṟu cāṉṟōr kūṟutal kāṇka. perumai uyarvu kuṟittu niṉṟatu. iṉiuravōrtam
   eṇṇiṉum maṭavōr tām eṇṇiṉum, irutiṟattōrum niṉṉaiyē uvamamākak
   koṇṭuraippa reṉṟumām.
   4-9 marutam........meymmaṟai
   urai: marutam cāṉṟa-maruta vaḷam amainta; malartalai viḷaivayaṟ
   ceyyuḷ-virinta iṭattaiyuṭaiya viḷai pulaṅkaḷākiya kaḻaṉikkaṇṇē yulavum;
   nārai oyyum makaḷir - nāraikaḷai yōppum makaḷir; iravum pakalum pāciḻai
   kaḷaiyār- iravu paka leṉṟa irupōtiṉum tāmaṇinta paciya poṉṉāṟ ceyta
   iḻaikaḷaik kaḷaiyārāy; kuṟumpal yāṇark kuravai yayarum- oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu
   aṇittāyp palavākiya iṭaṅkaḷilē putiya putiya kuravaikkūttiṉai yāṭi
   makiḻum; kāviri maṇṭiya-kāviri yāṟṟiṉ nīr mikakuḷḷa; cēyviri
   vaṉappiṉ-neṭun tolaivilēyē virintu tōṉṟum aḻakiṉaiyuṭaiya; pukārc
   celva- pukār nakarattaiyuṭaiya celvaṉē; pūḻiyar meymmaṟai-pūḻi
   nāṭṭārkku meypukukaruvi pōṉṟavaṉē e-ṟu
   marutattiṇaik kuriya poruḷpalavum kuṟaivaṟa niṟaintirukkumāṟu tōṉṟa,
   "marutañ cāṉṟa malartalai viḷaivayal" eṉṟum āṅkuṟaiyum uḻavarmakaḷir
   vayaṟkaṇ viḷaipayirai nāraikaḷ mitittuc citaikkāvaṇṇam ōppum
   ceykaiyiṉarātalai, "ceyyuḷ nārai yoyyum makaḷir", eṉṟum kūṟiṉār.
   vittiya voṉṟu āyiramāka viḷaitaliṉ celvak kaḷippāl iravu pakal eññāṉṟum
   viḷaiyāṭṭiṉai virumpi yoḻukukiṉṟā reṉpār, "iravum pakalum pāciḻai
   kaḷaiyār, kuṟumpala yāṇark kuravai yayarum" eṉṟār. paḻaiyavuraikārar,
   "iravum pakalum kuravai yayaru meṉak kūṭṭuka veṉṟum, "maruta meṉṟatu
   marutanilat taṉmaiyai" yeṉṟum, "kuṟumpal kuravai yeṉṟatu oṉṟu āṭum
   iṭattiṟku oṉṟu aṇiyatāy avaitām palavā yirukkiṉṟa kuravai yeṉṟavāṟu"
   eṉṟum, "kuravai yayarum pukār eṉavum kāviri maṇṭiya pukār eṉavum
   kūṭṭuka" eṉṟum kūṟuvar.
   kāviri pāytalāl nīrvaḷam ciṟattaliṉ. vāṉaḷāva vuyarnta cōlaikaḷum
   koṭimāṭap perumaṉaikaḷum cēymaikkaṇṇē kāṇpārkku aḻakiya tamatu
   kāṭciyiṉai vaḻaṅkum ciṟappu viḷaṅka, "cēyviri vaṉappiṟ pukār" eṉṟum,
   ataṉāṟ celvamukuti tōṉṟa, "celva" eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. pūḻiyar, pūḻi nāṭṭār;
   innāṭum cērark kuriyatu; "palvēṟ pūḻiyar kōvē" (patiṟ.84) eṉṟu piṟarum
   cēravēntaraik kūṟutal kāṇka.
   10-11 kaḻai.........poṟaiya.
   urai: kaḻai virintu eḻutaru - mūṅkilkaḷ virinteḻukiṉṟa; maḻai tavaḻ
   neṭuṅkōṭṭu -mēkaṅkaḷ tavaḻum neṭiya ucciyaiyuṭaiya; kollip poruna -
   kollimalaikkut talaivaṉē; koṭittērp poṟaiya - koṭi yuyarttiya
   tērkaḷaiyuṭaiya malaināṭṭaracē e-ṟu.
   mūṅkilkaḷ virintu vaḷarum iyalpiṉavātaliṉ "virinteḻutaru" eṉṟār. kolli,
   kollimalai; itaṉaic cūḻnta nāṭu kollikkūṟṟa meṉṟum vaḻaṅkum. poṟai,
   malai. cēranāṭu malaināṭātaliṉ, cērar poṟaiya reṉavum kūṟappaṭuvar.
   11-17 niṉ vaḷaṉum...yāṉē
   urai: niṉ vaḷaṉum āṇmaiyum kaivaṇmaiyum māntar aḷaviṟantaṉa eṉa -
   niṉṉuṭaiya celvamum vīramum koṭaiyum makkaḷ ārāycci yellaiyaik
   kantaṉavākum eṉṟu; yāṉ paṉṉāḷ ceṉṟu uraippavum tēṟār - yāṉ pala nāḷum
   ceṉṟu uraittēṉākavum niṉ pakaivar teḷiyārāyiṉār; piṟarum cāṉṟōr
   uraippat teḷikuvar kolleṉa - piṟarākiya cāṉṟōraikkoṇṭu terivippiṉ avar
   teḷivareṉa muyaṉṟavaḻiyum; āṅkum matimaruḷak kāṇkuval -appōḻtum avar
   aṟivu mayaṅki yiruppataiyē kaṇṭēṉ; yāṅku uraippēṉ eṉa yāṉ varuntuval -
   ataṉāl avarkaṭku evvāṟu kūṟit teḷivikkamuṭiyu meṉṟu niṉaintu
   varuntukiṉṟēṉ e-ṟu.
   vaḷam mutaliyaṉa muṟaiyē poruḷvali, taṉ tōḷvali, tuṇai tōtiya vāṟṟai,
   "māntar aḷaviṟantaṉa veṉap paṉṉāḷ yāṉceṉ vali paṭaivali mutaliyavaṟṟaic
   cuṭṭi niṟṟalāl ivaṟṟait tām eṭuttuṟuraippavum" eṉṟār. orunāḷoḻiyiṉum
   paṉṉāḷuraippiṉ aṟivu teḷikuva reṉṉum tuṇivāl, "paṉṉāḷ ceṉṟu uraippavum
   tēṟār" eṉṟār. "uraippavum eṉṟa ummai tāmē aṟiyakkaṭavataṉai yām
   collavum aṟikilareṉac ciṟappummai" eṉṟum, "piṟaruma eṉṟa vummai
   acainilai" yeṉṟum paḻaiyavuraikārar kūṟuvar. kūṟappaṭum poruḷai nōkkātu
   kūṟuvōr takuti nōkkum melliyar pōlum eṉṟu karutip piṟarākiya cāṉṟōrai
   viṭuttavaḻiyum, avarpāl mayakkamē pulappaṭuvatāyiṟ ṟeṉpār, ”āṅkum
   matimaruḷak kāṇkuval" eṉṟum, attuṇai maṭamai niṟaintōrai oṟuppatu
   aṟamākātākaliṉ, avar poruṭṭu iraṅkuvatallatu piṟitoṉṟum ceytaṟ
   kiṉmaiyiṉ, "varuntuval yāṉē" eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. yāṅkuraippēṉeṉa mīṭṭum
   avarkaṭkut takuvaṉa kūṟit teruṭṭaṟkaṇ tam viḻaiviruppattaip
   pulappaṭuttuvataṉāl, avarpāl aruḷalaiyē vēṇṭiṉā reṉpatu karuttāyiṟṟu.
   "cāṉṟōr uraippat teḷiruvar kolleṉa eṉṟataṉpiṉ, karutiṉ eṉa oru col
   varuvittu ataṉaik kāṇkuva leṉṉum viṉaiyoṭu muṭikka" eṉpar
   paḻaiyavuraikārar.
   itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyatu: oru peru vēntē, pukārc celva, pūḻiyar meymmaṟai,
   kollip poruna, koṭittērp poṟaiya, niṉ vaḷaṉum āṇmaiyum kai vaṇmaiyum
   māntar aḷaviṟantaṉa eṉap paṉṉāḷ ceṉṟu yāṉ uraippavum, niṉ pakaivar
   tēṟār; piṟa cāṉṟōruraippat teḷikuvar kolleṉa muyaṉṟavaḻi āṅkum mati
   maruḷak kāṇkuval; yāṅkuraippēṉ eṉa varuntuval; ākavē avarai aṟiyāmai
   yuṭaiyar eṉṟeṇṇi aruḷutal vēṇṭum eṉṟu muṭikka; iṉip paḻaiyavurai, "peru
   vēntē, pukārc celva, pūḻiyar meymmaṟai, kollip poruna, poṟaiya, niṉ
   pakaivar niṉ vaḷaṉum āṇmaiyum kai vaṇmaiyum ulakattu makkaḷ aḷavaik
   kaṭantaṉa; avaṉoṭu māṟupaṭuvatu numakku uṟutiyaṉṟeṉap paṉṉāḷ yāṉ
   collavum tēṟiṟṟilar; tēṟārāyiṉum ulakattu matippuṭaiya cāṉṟōr collat
   tām tēṟuvarō veṉak karutiṉ avar coṉṉaviṭattum avarkaḷ mati maruṇṭatumē
   kāṇāniṉṟēṉ; ākalāṉ niṉ perumaiyai avarkaṭku yāṅkuraippēṉeṉa
   varuntāniṉṟēṉ yāṉ; iḵtu eṉṉuṟu kuṟai; itaṉai yaṟintu nī avarpāl aruḷa
   vēṇṭuvaleṉa viṉaimuṭivu ceyka," eṉṟu kūṟum.
   "itaṉāṟ colliyatu, avaṉ veṉṟikku aṭiyākiya celvamum āṇmaiyum kai
   vaṇmaiyum uṭaṉkūṟiyavāṟṟāṉ avaṉ veṉṟic ciṟappuk kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu"
   ----------
                     ___________________________________

                        8.4. nalam peṟu tirumaṇi

   74.
   kēḷvi kēṭṭup paṭiva moṭiyātu
       vēḷvi vēṭṭaṉai yuyarntō ruvappac
       cāyaṟal kaṭukkun tāḻiruṅ kūntal
       vēṟupaṭu tiruvi ṉiṉvaḻi vāḻiyar
       koṭumaṇam paṭṭa viṉaimā ṇaruṅkalam         5
       pantarp payanta palarpukaḻ muttam
       varaiyaka naṇṇik kuṟumpoṟai nāṭit
       teriyunar koṇṭa ciraṟuṭaip paimpoṟik
       kavaimaraṅ kaṭukkuṅ kavalaiya maruppiṟ
       puḷḷi yiralait tōlū ṉutirttut         10
       tītukaḷain teñciya tikaḻviṭu pāṇṭiṟ
       paruti pōkiya puṭaikiḷai kaṭṭi
       eḵkuṭai yirumpi ṉuḷḷamaittu vallōṉ
       cūṭu nilaiyuṟṟuc cuṭarviṭu tōṟṟam
       vicumpāṭu marapiṟ paruntū ṟaḷappa         15
       nalampeṟu tirumaṇi kūṭṭu naṟṟōḷ
       oṭuṅkī rōti yoṇṇutal karuvil
       eṇṇiyaṉ muṟṟi yīraṟivu purintu
       cālpum cemmaiyu muḷappaṭap piṟavum
       kāvaṟ kamainta varacutuṟai pōkiya         20
       vīṟucāl putalvaṟ peṟṟaṉai yivaṇark
       karuṅkaṭa ṉiṟutta ceruppukaṉ muṉpa
       aṉṉavai maruṇṭeṉa ṉallē ṉiṉvayiṉ
       muḻutuṇarn toḻukku naraimū tāḷaṉai
       vaṇmaiyu māṇpum vaḷaṉu meccamum         25
       teyvamum yāvatun tavamuṭai yōrkkeṉa
       vēṟupaṭu naṉantalaip peyarak
       **ṟiṉai perumaniṉ paṭimai yāṉē.

   ituvamatu.
   peyar nalampeṟu tirumaṇi.
   3-17. cāyaṟal . . . . . . .karuvil
   urai: cāy aṟal kaṭukkum-nuṇṇiya karumaṇalai yokkum; tāḻ iruṅ
   kūntal-tāḻnta kariya kūntalaiyum; koṭu maṇam paṭṭa viṉaimāṇ
   aruṅkalam-koṭumaṇam eṉṉum ūrilē uṇṭākiya ariya kalaṉkaḷaiyum; pantar
   payanta palar pukaḻ muttam-pantar eṉṉum ūriṭattē peṟṟa palarum
   pukaḻkiṟa muttukkaḷaiyum; varai yakam naṇṇi-peru malaikaḷai yaṭaintu
   avaṟṟiṭattum tēṭi; teriyunar koṇṭa- kuṟṟamaṟa ārāyntukoḷḷum aṟiviṉai
   yuṭaiyōr koṇṭa; ciraṟuṭaip paimpoṟi-citaṟik kiṭattalaiyuṭaiya paciya
   poṟikaḷaiyum;kavaimaram kaṭukkuma; kavalaiya maruppiṉ-kavaikkōṟ pōlak
   kiḷaittalaiyuṭaiya kompiṉaiyum; puḷḷi iralait tōl-puḷḷiyiṉaiyumuṭaiya
   māṉtōlaik koṇṭu: ūṉ utirttu-ataṉ uṭpuṟattut tacaiyaik kaḷaintu: tītu
   kaḷaintu-ēṉai aḻukkukaḷaiyum pōkki; eñciya tikaḻviṭu- eñci niṉṟa oḷi
   vīcukiṉṟa; pāṇṭiṟ paruti pōkiya puṭai- vaṭṭamāka aṟutta tōliṉ
   viḷimpilē; kiḷai kaṭṭi- iṉamākac cērak kaṭṭi; eḵkuṭai
   irumpiṉ-kūrmaiyaiyuṭaiya vūciyāl ; vallōṉ-toḻil vallavaṉ; uḷḷamaittu-
   uṭpuṟattē maṇikaḷaip patittu aḻakuṟa aṇi ceytu; cūṭu
   nilaiyuṟṟu-cūṭutaṟkuriya nilaimaiyiṉai yuṟuvittu; cuṭarviṭu tōṟṟam-oḷi
   tikaḻkiṉṟa tōṟṟattaip peṟuvittalāl; vicum pāṭu marapiṉ-vicumpilē
   paṟakkum muṟaimaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya; paruntu ūṟu aḷappa-paruntāṉatu ūṉeṉṟu
   karuti yataṉaiyuṟṟuk kavartaṟku niṉaiyumāṟu; nalam peṟu tirumaṇi-nalam
   peṟṟa uyariya at tōṟpaṇiyākiya maṇiyai; kūṭṭum naṟṟōḷ-aṇikiṉṟa nalla
   tōḷiṉaiyum; oṭuṅkirōti-oṭuṅkiya curuḷ eṉṉum pakutiyāṉa kūntalaiyum;
   oṇṇutal oḷḷiya neṟṟiyiṉaiyum; vēṟupaṭu tiruviṉ-piṟappāl tirumakaḷiṉ
   vēṟupaṭṭā ḷeṉṉum ciṟappeytiya māṇpiṉaiyumuṭaiya niṉ tēviyiṉuṭaiya;
   niṉvaḻi vāḻiyar-niṉ kuṭivaḻi nīṭu vāḻvataṉ poruṭṭu; karuvil-karuviṭattē
   yamaintu e-ṟu
   meṉmaiyum nuṇmaiyu muṭaimaipaṟṟik karumaṇalai"cāyaṟal" eṉal vēṇṭiṟṟu.
   tāḻ iruṅ kūntal, mutukiṭattē tāḻnta kariya kūntal;"puṟantāḻ piruḷiya
   piṟaṅkukura laimpāl" (akam 126) eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka.
   kūntalaiyum, tōḷiṉaiyum nutaliṉaiyumuṭaiya tēviyiṉatu karuvil eṉak
   kūṭṭuka. tāḻiruṅ kūntaleṉṟavar maṟupaṭiyum oṭuṅkīrōti yeṉṟatu, kūntaliṉ
   nīṭciyum niṟappolivum potuppaṭak kūṟi, ataṉaic curuḷ eṉṉum pakutiyāka
   muṭikkum ciṟap piṉai yuṇarttaṟku. tirumakaṭkuriya uruvum piṟa
   nalaṅkaḷum poruntip piṟappoṉṟiṉāl vēṟupaṭuvatu kuṟittut tēviyai,
   "vēṟupaṭutiru" eṉṟār. vēṟupaṭu tiruviṉ eṉpataṟkup
   paḻaiyavuraikārar"vēṟupaṭu tiruviṉ eṉṟatu ivaḷukkuk kūṟiya kuṇaṅkaḷāl
   avaḷiṉ vēṟākiya niṉ tēvi eṉṟavā" ṟeṉṟum, "tiruviṉ eṉṉum iṉ aṟainilai"
   yeṉṟum kūṟuvar
   koṭumaṇam, pantar eṉpaṉa akkālattē ciṟappuṟṟirunta ūrkaḷ. viḷaimāṇ
   aruṅkalam, toḻil nalattāl māṇpuṟṟa aṇikalaṉkaḷ. palar, palanāṭṭavar.
   vēḷvi ceytaṟkaṇ, ataṉaic ceyvōr nallilakkaṇam amainta puḷḷimāṉiṉ tōlait
   tūymai ceytu pōrttukkoḷpa vātaliṉ, ataṉai, māṉilakkaṇam terintāraik
   koṇṭu peṟutal vēṇṭi viṭuttaliṉ, avarait"teriyunar" eṉṟum, avarkaḷ
   ataṉai nāṭip peṟumāṟu kūṟuvār, "varaiyakam naṇṇik kuṟumpoṟai nāṭit
   teriyunar" eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. varaiyakam, perumalait toṭar. kuṟumpeṟai,
   ciṟu kuṉṟukaḷ niṟainta malaippakkam. koccināṭṭup pakutikaḷai iṭaikkālak
   kal veṭṭukkaḷ neṭumpoṟai nāṭeṉṟum kuṟum poṟai nāṭeṉṟum (A.R. No. 321 of
   1924) kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. ivviṭattai naṇṇi nāṭutaliṉ, uyariya māṉ vakaikaḷ
   avviṭattē vāḻtal peṟṟām. nallilakkaṇam amainta māṉiṉ nalam kūṟuvār.,
   kiḷaitta kompuṭaimaiyum, uṭaleṅkum citaṟiya puḷḷiyuṭaimaiyum vitantu,
   "paimpoṟik kavaimaram kaṭukkum kavalaiya maruppiṟ puḷḷiyiralai"
   yeṉṟār."varaiyeṉṟatu perumalaiyai" yeṉṟum, kuṟumpoṟai yeṉṟatu"ataṉai
   yaṇainta ciṟu poṟṟaikaḷai" yeṉṟum, "teriyunar kōṭal, ilakkaṇakkuṟṟamaṟa
   ārāyntu kōṭal" eṉṟum, "paim poṟi yeṉṟatu cevvikaḷaiyuṭaiya puḷḷikaḷai"
   yeṉṟum"mēṟpuḷḷi yiralai yeṉṟataṉai ataṉ cātip peyar kūṟiyavāṟākak
   koḷka" eṉṟum paḻaiyavurai kūṟum.
   ciraṟutal, citaṟutal. kavalai, kavarttal vēḷviceyvōṉ, māṉ tōlaip
   pōrttukkoḷḷa, avaṉ maṉaivi attōlai vaṭṭamāka aṟuttu ataṉaic cuṟṟilum
   muttukkaḷaiyum, piṟa vuyariya maṇikaḷaiyum kaṭṭi, naṭuvē uyariya
   māṇikkamaṇikaḷait taittuk tōḷiṭattē aṇivaḷ pōlum, īṇṭu āciriyar
   aracamātēvi ivvāṟu aṇintāḷeṉṟaliṉ, mayir mutaliyaṉa mūṭip poliviṉṟi
   yirukkum pakutiyait tītu eṉa vilakkutalāl, "tītu kaḷainteñciya tikaḻ
   viṭu pāṇṭiṟ paruti" eṉṟum vaṭṭamāṉa tōlaip"pāṇṭiṟ paruti" yeṉṟum, ataṉ
   viḷimpilē koṭumaṇattuk kalattaiyum pantariṭattup peṟṟa muttiṉaiyum iṉam
   cērak kaṭṭiṉāreṉṟaṟku, "puṭaikiḷai kaṭṭi" yeṉṟum,uḷḷiṭattē tāmarai
   mutaliya pūvaip pōlum aḻakiya vēlaippāṭṭiṉaic civanta māṇikkamaṇi koṇṭu
   ceyyum toḻil vallunarai, "vallōṉ eḵkuṭai yirumpiṉ uḷḷamaittu" eṉṟum
   avvāṟu amaittalāl cūṭutaṟkuriya nilaiyuṟuvatāl, "cūṭunilaiyuṟṟu" eṉṟum
   kūṟiṉār.
   uṟuvitteṉappālatu"uṟṟu" eṉa vantatu. civanta maṇikaḷ iṭaiyē
   patikkappeṟṟuc cevvoḷi kāṉṟu tikaḻtal kaṇṭu, ūṉeṉṟu karutip paruntu
   kavarntucellak karutumeṉpār, "vicumpāṭu marapiṟ paruntu ṟaḷappa" eṉṟār.
   ūṉ tēṭi yuṟṟuṇṭu payiṉṟa kūriya pārvaiyiṉai yuṭaiya paruntiṉai
   mayakkumāṟu civanta oḷikoṇṭu viḷaṅkum maṇiyātalpaṟṟi, "nalam peṟu
   tirumaṇi" eṉap paṭṭatu. poṉṉiṭaip patittup polivuṟuttap peṟum māṇikka
   maṇi, tōliṭaip patittavaḻiyum taṉ nalloḷi kuṉṟātu tikaḻum ciṟappuk
   kuṟittu ivvāṟu nalampeṟu tirumaṇiyeṉṟa nalattāl, ip pāṭṭiṟku nalampeṟu
   tirumaṇiyeṉṟu peyarāyiṟṟeṉa vaṟika. iṉip paḻaiyavuraikārar, "nalampeṟu
   tirumaṇi yeṉṟatu maṇiyaṟivārāl ituvē nallateṉṟu collappaṭutalaiyuṭaiya
   tirumaṇi yeṉṟavāṟu; icciṟappāṉē itaṟku nalampeṟu tirumaṇi yeṉṟu
   peyarāyiṟṟu" eṉpar.
   itaṉkaṭ kūṟiyavaṟṟait tokuttu nōkkiṉ, tāḻiruṅ kūntalaiyum, tirumaṇi
   kūṭṭum naṟṟōḷaiyum, ōtiyaiyum, oṇṇutalaiyum, vēṟupaṭu
   tiruviṉaiyaiyumuṭaiya niṉ tēviyiṉ karuvilē eṉpatām. iṉiyip pakutikkaṇ
   vantavaṟṟiṟku uraik kuṟippuk kūṟaluṟṟa paḻaiya vuraikārar, "kavalaiya
   veṉṉum akaram ceyyuḷ vikāra" meṉṟum, pāṇṭi leṉṟatu vaṭṭamāka aṟutta
   tōliṉai yeṉṟum, "paruti pōkiya puṭai yeṉṟatu vaṭṭamākap pōṉa attōlatu
   viḷimpiṉai" yeṉṟum, eḵkuṭai yirumpiṉ uḷḷamait teṉṟatu kūrmaiyaiyuṭaiya
   karuvikaḷāl attōluṭ ceyyum toḻilkaḷellām ceytamait teṉṟavā" ṟeṉṟum,
   amaitteṉṟataṉai"amaippaveṉat tirikka" veṉṟum, vallōṉāl eṉa virittu
   vallōṉāl niṉ tēvi cūṭutal nilaiyuṟutalāl eṉka" eṉṟum, "vallōṉ yākam
   paṇṇuvikka vallavaṉ" eṉṟum, "tōṟṟameṉṟatu tōṟṟamuṭaiya attōliṉai"
   yeṉṟum kūṟuvar. karuvil eṉpataṉōṭu amainteṉa oru col varuvittu,
   vāḻireṉṟa muṟṟeccattukku muṭipākkātu, "vāḻiyareṉṉum viṉai yeccattiṉaic
   cūṭu nilaiyuṟṟu eṉṉum viṉaiyoṭu muṭikka" eṉpar paḻaiyavuraikārar.
   18-21. eṇṇiyal................peṟṟaṉai.
   urai: eṇ iyal muṟṟi-eṇṇappaṭukiṉṟa tiṅkaḷ pattum nirampi; īr aṟivu
   pirintu-iruvakai yaṟivum amaintu; cālpum cemmaiyum uḷappaṭap
   piṟavum-cālpum naṭuvunilaiyu muḷḷiṭṭa piṟa naṟpaṇpukaḷum; kāvaṟ
   kamainta aracu tuṟai pōkiya vīṟucāl-nāṭukāttaṟku vēṇṭum araciya laṟivu
   vakai palavum muṟṟak kaṟṟut tuṟaipōkiya ciṟappum niṟainta; putal vaṟ
   peṟṟaṉai ivaṇarkku-naṉmakaṉaip peṟṟuḷḷāy innila vulakattu vāḻvār
   poruṭṭu e-ṟu.
   karuvaḷartaṟkuriya kālattai ovvoru tiṅkaḷāka veṇṇupavātaliṉ, "eṇṇiyal
   muṟṟi" yeṉṟār. īraṟivu, iruvakai yaṟivu; avai iyaṟkaiyum
   ceyaṟkaiyumākiya iruvakai yaṟivukaḷ. iyaṟkaiyāya kaṇṇoḷi yillārkku ēṉai
   ñāyiṟṟoḷi mutaliyaṉa payaṉ paṭāmaipōla, iyaṟkaiyē uṇmaiyaṟivillārkkuk
   kalvi kēḷvikaḷāṉām ceyaṟkaiyaṟivu payaṉpaṭā tākaliṉ, "īraṟivu purintu"
   eṉṟāreṉṟumām. immai maṟumai yaṟivukaḷumām. periya aṟiveṉiṉumamaiyu
   meṉpar paḻaiyavuraikārar. cālpu, naṟkuṇaṅkaḷiṉ niṟaivu. aṉpu, nāṇ,
   oppuṟavu, kaṇṇōṭṭam, vāymai eṉṟa aintiṉ niṟaivu. aracāḷum tiṟaṉum
   karuvilē vāykkun tiruveṉṟaṟku, "kāvaṟkamainta aracu tuṟai pōkiya vīṟu"
   eṉṟār. piṟanta piṉpē ivai yāvum peṟaṟ kuriyavāyiṉum, ivaṟṟiṉ pēṟu iṉitu
   poruntutaṟkuriya nalvāyppuk karuvilē uṇṭāvatākaliṉ, ivvāṟu kūṟiṉār eṉa
   aṟika. ikkaruttē, "peṟumavaṟṟuḷ yāmaṟiva tillai aṟivaṟinta makkaṭpē
   ṟalla piṟa" (kuṟaḷ 61") eṉpuḻiyum amaintukiṭattal kāṇka. peṟṟaṉai
   yeṉpataip paḻaiyavuraikārar, muṟṟeccamākki mēl varuvaṉavaṟṟōṭu
   muṭippar. aracaṉatu putalvaṟ pēṟu avaṉ kōṟkīḻ vāḻvārkku ēmamātalpaṟṟi,
   "ivaṇark" keṉṟār. makkaṭ pēṟu, peṟṟōrkkē yaṉṟi ip perunilattu vāḻvārkku
   nalam payakkum iyalpiṟ ṟeṉpatu paṇṭaittamiḻ naṉmakkaḷ koḷkai."tam
   miṉtam makkaḷ aṟivuṭaimai mānilattu, maṉṉuyirkkellām iṉitu" (kuṟaḷ 68)
   eṉṟu cāṉṟōr kūṟutal kāṇka.
   1-2 kēḷvi...........uvappa
   urai: kēḷvi kēṭṭu-arumaṟaip poruḷai aṟivr uraippak kēṭṭu; paṭivam
   oṭiyātu-avar uraitta virataṅkaḷai mēṟkoṇṭu tavirātoḻuki; uyarntōr
   uvappa- aṟivu oḻukkaṅkaḷāl uyarnta naṉmakkaḷ maṉamakiḻumpaṭi; vēḷvi
   vēṭṭaṉai- vēḷvikaḷaic ceytu muṭittāy e-ṟu
   arumaṟaipporuḷai oruvar tāmē kaṟṟuṇartal kūṭāmaiyiṉ, "kēḷvi kēṭṭu"
   eṉṟār. kēḷvi, arumaṟaipporuḷ; kēṭṭaṟkuriyatu atuvākaliṉ, kēṭṭa poruḷai
   naṭaimuṟaiyil teḷintu nallaṟivu kaivarap peṟutaṟku virata voḻukkam
   vēṇṭiyiruttalāl, "paṭiva moṭiyātu" eṉṟār. kēṭṭavaṟṟai maṉattāl
   oṉṟiyirun tuṇartaṟkut tuṇaiceyvatu paṭivameṉa vuṇarka. vēḷvippayaṉ uyar
   nilai yoḻukkattuc cāṉṟōrkku uvakai payappippa tātalāl, "uyarn tōruvappa
   vēḷvi vēṭṭaṉai" yeṉṟār."āṉṟa kēḷvi yaṭaṅkiya koḷkai, nāṉmaṟai mutalvar
   cuṟṟamāka, maṉṉa rēval ceyya maṉṉiya, vēḷvi muṟṟiya vāyvāḷ vēntē"
   (puṟam 26) eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka.
   iṉi, paḻaiyavuraikārar, "kēḷvi kēṭṭal-yākam paṇṇutaṟ kuṭalāṉa viti
   kēṭṭal; paṭivam-yākam paṇṇutaṟku uṭalāka muṉpu celuttum virataṅkaḷ.
   oṭiyāteṉpatai oṭiyāmal eṉat tirikka" eṉṟum, "vēṭṭaṉai eṉṟataṉai
   viṉaiyeccamuṟṟākki avviṉaiyec cattiṉai aruṅkaṭaṉ iṟutta eṉṉum viṉaiyoṭu
   kūṭṭuka" eṉṟum"uyarntōr tēva" reṉṟum kūṟuvar.
   22-28. aruṅkaṭaṉ..............paṭimaiyāṉē
   urai: aruṅkaṭaṉ iṟutta-in nilavulakattu vāḻvārkku aracarāyiṉār
   ceytaṟkuriya ariya kaṭaṉkaḷaic ceytu muṭitta; ceruppukal muṉpa- pōrai
   virumpum valiyiṉai yuṭaiyōy; niṉvayiṉ aṉṉavai maruṇṭaṉeṉ allēṉ-niṉpāl
   kēḷviyum vēḷviyum putalvaṟpēṟumākiya avaiyiṟṟaik kaṇṭu viyappuṟṟē
   ṉallēṉ;muḻutuṇarn toḻukkum narai mūtāḷaṉai-uṇarat takuvaṉavaṟṟai
   muḻutum uṇarntu piṟarkkum uṇartti naṉṉeṟi yoḻukappaṇṇum naraiyum
   mutumaiyumuṭaiya purōkitaṉai; niṉ paṭimaiyāṉ-nī mēṟkoṇṭirukkum tava
   voḻukkattāl; vaṇmaiyum māṇpum vaḷaṉum eccamum teyvamum yāvatum tavam
   uṭaiyōrkku eṉa-koṭaiyum kuṇavamaitiyum celvamum makappēṟum
   teyvavuṇarvum piṟavum muṉṉait tavamuṭaiyārkkē yuḷavāvaṉa eṉa
   aṟivuṟutti; vēṟupaṭu taṉantalai peyarak kūṟiṉai-nāṭṭiṉ vēṟupaṭṭa akaṉṟa
   iṭamākiya kāṭṭiṟkut tavaṅ kuṟittuc cellap paṇittaṉai.; peruma-perumāṉē;
   itu kaṇṭaṉṟē yāṉ maruṭkai yeytuvē ṉāyiṉēṉ e-ṟu.
   aruṅkaṭaṉ iṟutta muṉpa, ceruppukal muṉpa eṉa iyaiyum. makappēṟṟāl
   ivaṇaraiyum, vēḷvi vēṭṭalāl uyartōraiyum, ōmputalpaṟṟi"aruṅkaṭaṉ
   iṟutta" eṉṟum, cerumēmpaṭṭārk kaṉṟi ivvaruṅkaṭaṉ eḷitil iṟukkalākāmai
   tōṉṟa, "ceruppukal muṉpa" eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. uyar poruḷai yōti yuṇarntum
   piṟark kuraittum tāṉoḻuka vallavaṉ eṉṟaṟkup purōkitaṉai"muḻutuṇarn
   toḻukkum naraimūtāḷaṉai" eṉṟār. naraippum mutumaiyumaṉṟi, vaṇmaiyum
   māṇpu mutalāyiṉa avaṉpāl peruka uḷavātal vēṇṭic cēramāṉ irakkaṅkoṇṭu
   avaṟṟaip peṟumāṟu aṟivukkoṭai vaḻaṅkiṉāṉ eṉpār, "vaṇmaiyum māṇpum
   vaḷaṉum eccamum teyvamum yāvatum tavamuṭaiyōrkku eṉa" aṟivuṟuttiṉāṉ
   eṉṟār. eccam pukaḻkkurittāyiṉum, vaṇmaiyum māṇpum kūṟavē ap pukaḻum
   avaṟṟiṉpāl aṭaṅkutalāl makkaṭpēṟāyiṟṟu. iṉip paḻaiyavuraikārar"
   naraimūtāḷaṉai yeṉṟatu purōkitaṉai" yeṉṟum"naraimūtāḷaṉaik kūṟiṉai
   yeṉak kūṭṭi avaṉaic colvi yēviṉaiyeṉa oru col varuvittu muṭikka" eṉṟum,
   "māṇpeṉṟatu māṭcimaiyuṭaiya kuṇaṅkaḷai" yeṉṟum, "eccameṉṟatu
   piḷḷappēṟṟiṉai" yeṉṟum, "teyvameṉṟatu tammāl vaḻipaṭum teyvattiṉai:
   yeṉṟum, "tavamuṭaiyōrkkeṉac colli yeṉka" eṉṟum"kūṟiṉai yeṉ patu
   viṉaiyeccamuṟ" ṟeṉṟum"vēṟupaṭu naṉantalai yeṉṟatu tuṟantu pōyirukkum
   kāṭṭiṉai" yeṉṟum."peyara veṉṟatu annarai mūtāḷaṉaik kāṭṭilē peyara
   vēṇṭi" yeṉṟum kūṟuvar.
   purōkitaṉ ceṉṟa kāṭṭiṉai"vēṟupaṭu naṉantalai" yeṉṟār, mūmpoṟikaḷāṉum
   āra nukarum nukarccikkiṭaṉākum nāṭṭiṉum avaṟṟai yaṭakkutaṟkut
   tuṇaiyākum ciṟappuppaṟṟi vēṟupaṭutaliṉ, cēramāṉ, ivvāṟu taṉ paṭimaiyāṉ
   purōkitaṉait tuṟavu mēṟkoḷḷap paṇittamai nōkkiṉ, ap purōkitaṉ
   āṉṟavintaṭaṅkum cālpiṟkuṟai paṭṭamaiyum, ataṉai niṟaittukkōṭaṟku vēntaṉ
   kaṇṇōṭi vēṟupaṭu naṉantalaip peyarttamaiyum peṟṟām. itu kaṇṭaṉṟē
   yeṉpatu mutalāyiṉa kuṟippeccam.
   itukāṟum kūṟiyatu: ceruppukal muṉpa, peruma, tāḻiruṅ kūn talaiyum,
   naṟṟōḷaiyum, ōtiyaiyum, nutalaiyum, tiruviṉaiyu muṭaiya niṉ tēviyiṉ
   karuvilē, iyal muṟṟi, aṟivu purintu, aracu tuṟai pōkiya putalvaṟ
   peṟṟaṉai ivaṇar poruṭṭu; uyarntōruvappa vēḷvi vēṭṭaṉai; aṉṉavai kaṇṭu
   maruṇṭaṉeṉallēṉ; narai mūtāḷaṉai, niṉpaṭimaiyāṉ, vēṟupaṭu naṉanlaip
   peyarak kūṟiṉai; itukaṇṭaṉṟē maruṭkai yeytuvēṉāyiṉēṉ eṉpatām. iṉip
   paḻaiyavurai, "vēṭṭaṉai yeṉṟum peṟṟaṉai yeṉṟum nī ceyta yākaṅkaḷākiya
   aṉṉavaiyiṟṟiṟku yāṉ maruṇṭēṉallēṉ, niṉṉai nalvaḻi yoḻukuvittu niṉṟa
   narai mūtāḷaṉai niṉpaṭimaiyāṉē illaṟa voḻukkiṉai yoḻittut tuṟavaṟa
   voḻukkilē cella oḻukuvittaṉai; avvāṟu ceyvitta niṉ pēroḻukkattaiyum
   pēraṟiviṉaiyum terintu yāṉ maruṇṭēṉeṉa viṉai muṭivu ceyka" eṉṟu kūṟum.
   "itaṉāṟ colliyatu, avaṉ nalloḻukkamum ataṟkēṟṟa nallaṟivuṭaimaiyum
   kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu.
   --------------
                     ___________________________________

                        8.5. tīñcēṟṟiyāṇar.

   75.
   irumpuli koṉṟu peruṅkaḷi ṟaṭūum
       arumpoṟi vayamā ṉaṉaiyai palvēṟ
       polantār yāṉai yiyaṟērp poṟaiya
       vēntarum vēḷirum piṟarum kīḻppaṇintu
       5 niṉvaḻip paṭāa rāyiṉeṉmikku)         5
       aṟaiyuṟu karumpiṉ tīñcēṟṟi yāṇar
       varunar varaiyā vaḷamvīṅ kirukkai
       vaṉpulan taḻīiya meṉpā ṟōṟum
       marupula viṉaiñar pulavikal paṭuttuk*
       10 kaḷḷuṭai niyamat toḷvilai koṭukkum         10
       veḷvara kuḻuta koḷḷuṭaik karampaic
       cennel valci yaṟiyār tattam
       pāṭal cāṉṟa vaippiṉ
       nāṭuṭa ṉāṭal yāvaṇa tavarkkē.

   ituvumatu
   peyar: tīñcēṟṟiyāṇar.
   1-3. irumpuli.........poṟaiya.
   urai: pal vēl polantār yāṉai iyal tērp poṟaiya-palavākiya vēṟpaṭaiyum
   poṉṉarimālai yaṇinta yāṉaiyum iyalukiṉṟa tēru muṭaiya peruñ cēralirum
   poṟaiyē; irumpuli koṉṟu-periya puliyaik koṉṟu; peruṅ kaḷiṟu
   aṭuum-ataṉālum cōrvaṭaiyātu uṭaṉ ceṉṟu periya yāṉaiyaik kollukiṉṟa;
   arum poṟi vayamāṉ aṉaiyai- ariya varikaḷaiyum valiyaiyumuṭaiya
   arimāviṉai oppāy;
   peruṅ kaḷiṟṟiṉum irumpuli vali mikavuṭaittātaliṉ, ataṉaik koṉṟum
   cōrvaṭaiyātē iṭaiyaṟaviṉṟip peruṅ kaḷiṟṟiṉaiyum kollum peruvaliyuṭaimai
   tōṉṟa, "irumpuli koṉṟu peruṅ kaḷiṟu aṭūum" eṉṟār. arimāviṟkup
   poṟaiyaṉaiyum pulikku ēṉai vēntaraiyum peruṅkaḷiṟṟukku vēḷir
   mutalāyiṉāraiyum koḷka. polantār, poṉṉarimālai.
   4-7vēntarum........vīṅkirukkai.
   urai: vēntarum vēḷirum piṟarum kīḻppaṇintu niṉ vaḻip paṭāa
   rāyiṉ-muṭivēntarum kuṟunila maṉṉarum piṟarum niṉṉaik kīḻppaṇintu niṉ
   viruppiṉvaḻi oḻukārāyiṉ; nel mikku-nel mikku viḷaiya; aṟaiyuṟu karumpiṉ
   tīñcēṟṟu yāṇar-ataṟ kiṭaiyūṟāka vaḷarntu muṟṟiyiruttalpaṟṟi veṭṭappaṭṭa
   karumpiṉatu tīviya cāṟākiya putu varuvāyiṉai; varunar varaiyara vaḷam
   vīṅku irukkai-avviṭattē varuvōrkku varaiyātu vaḻaṅkum celvam mikunta
   irukkaikaḷ (ūrkaḷ) eṉka. nelmikku viḷaitalāl ataṉaiyum karumpiṉ tīñ
   cēṟu mēṉ
   mēlum perukutalāl ataṉaiyum varuvōrkku varaiyātu vaḻaṅkupa eṉpatām.
   aṟaiyuṟu karumpu, aṟuttalaip poruntiya karumpu; iṟuttal iṟaiyeṉa
   varutalpōla, aṟuttal aṟaiyeṉa vantatu; nellukku vēliyāka naṭṭa karumpu
   mika vaḷarntu nelliṉ vaḷarcciyaik keṭuttalāl, aṟukkappaṭṭa karumpu
   eṉṟaṟku"aṟaiyuṟu karumpu" eṉṟāreṉka. paḻaiyavuraikārarum, "nelmikku
   aṟaiyuṟu karum peṉṟatu nelliṉkaṇṇē annellai nerukki mika eḻuntamaiyāṉē
   aṟukkaluṟṟa karumpeṉṟavāṟu" eṉṟum, "iṉi anneṟṟāṉ karumpiṉ mika eḻuntu
   ataṉai nerukkiṉamaiyāl an nelliṟku iṭamuṇṭāka aṟutta karumpu eṉiṉumām."
   eṉṟum uraippar. varaiyātu vaḻaṅkiya vaḻiyum vaḷam kuṉṟāmaipaṟṟi,
   "varaiyā vaḷam vīṅkirukkai" eṉṟār. mikku eṉpatai mikaveṉat tirikka.
   --------------------
   *arumpaṟai viṉaiñar pullinal paṭuttu-pāṭa vēṟupāṭu.
   "tīñcēṟu iṉiya pāku" eṉṟum, yāṇareṉṟatu attīñcēṟṟatu iṭaiyaṟa
   liṉmaiyai" eṉṟum, "ic ciṟappāṉē itaṟkut tīñcēṟṟiyāṇar eṉṟu peyarāyiṟ"
   ṟeṉṟum paḻaiyavuraikārar kūṟuvar
   nellukku vēliyāka naṭṭa karumpu mika vaḷarntu ataṉ vaḷarccikku
   iṭaiyūṟāvatu kaṇṭu ataṉait taṭintupeṟṟa tīñcēṟṟiyāṇarai varunarkku
   varaiyātu vaḻaṅkuvareṉṟatu, kuṭi puṟantārātu avarkku iṭaiyūṟu viḷaikkum
   koṭuṅkōlaracai veṉṟu, avarpāṟ peṟṟa poruḷai varunarkku varaiyātu
   vaḻaṅkum cēramāṉatu naṟceyal uḷḷuṟut turaittavāṟu.
   8-14 vaṉpulam................tavarkkē
   urai: vaṉpulam taḻīiya meṉpāl tōṟum-vaṉṉilaṅkaḷaic cāravuḷḷa maruta
   nilaṅkaḷ tōṟum; marupula viṉaiñar- vittiyatu muḷaiyāta kaḷar nilattē
   toḻiliṉaic ceyyum maṟavar; pulavikal paṭuttu-am maruta vayalkaḷaik
   kōṭal kuṟittu nikaḻttum māṟupāṭṭaik keṭuttaḻittu; kaḷḷuṭai niyamattu
   oḷvilai koṭukkum-avarpāṟ peṟṟuk kaḷ viṟkum kaṭaikaḷil kaḷḷiṟku
   vilaiyākak koṭukkum; veḷvaraku uḻuta koḷḷuṭaik karampai-uḻutuvittiya
   veḷḷai varakum koḷḷum viḷaiyum karampaiyāy viṭutalāl; cennel valci
   aṟiyār-avvarakum koḷḷu mallatu cenneṟcōṟu peṟātu varuntupa vākaliṉ,
   avarkku-av vēntar mutalāyiṉārkku; tattam pāṭal cāṉṟa vaippiṉ
   nāṭu-tattammuṭaiya pulavar pāṭum pukaḻ amainta ūrkaḷaiyuṭaiya nāṭṭai;
   uṭaṉ āṭal yāvaṇatu -oruṅku āḷuvatu eṅṅaṉam kūṭum. e-ṟu
   eḷitil uḻutu vittutaṟkākāta vaṉṉilattaic cārntuḷḷa maruta vayalkaḷai,
   "vaṉpulan taḻīiya meṉpāl" eṉṟār. meṉpālil nalala viḷaivu uṇṭātaliṉ
   ataṉaik kōṭaṟku ēṉai vaṉpulattu vāḻum marunilattār virumpi muyalpa
   vātalālum, akkālai meṉpulattu vāḻnar avaroṭu porupa vātalālum, appōril
   tōṟkum marupula viṉaiñar tampāl uḷḷa varakum koḷḷum taṇṭamākat
   tarupavātalālum"marupula viṉaiñar pulavikal paṭuttu" eṉṟum, at taṇṭap
   poruḷum kaḷvilaikkē payaṉpaṭukiṟateṉpār" kaḷḷuṭai niyamattu oḷvilai
   koṭukkum veḷvara kuḻuta koḷ" eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. maru pulam,
   kaḷarnilam."marunila muḻutatil eru mikappeytu,vittiṭṭāṅkē viṉaipayaṉ
   koḷḷac, cittattuṉṉum mattarpōlavum" (tirukkaḻumala mummaṇi 22: 16-18)
   eṉap paṭṭiṉattaṭikaḷ kūṟuvatu kāṇka. vaṉ pulan taḻīiya meṉpāleṉṟaliṉ,
   vaṉpulattār meṉpulattiṉ mēl vaḻakkut toṭuppārātalāl, ataṉaip"pulavikal"
   eṉṟār. meṉpula viṉaiñarāl pulliyavākak karutappaṭum varakum koḷḷum
   vaṉpulattārkku oṇporuḷātalāl, kaḷḷiṟku avaṟṟai uyariya poruḷākak
   koṭuppa reṉpā, "oḷvilai koṭukkum" eṉṟār. avvaḷam vīṅku irukkaikaḷ
   veḷvarakum koḷḷum vittum karampaiyāy viṭutalāl"veḷvara kuḻuta koḷḷuṭaik
   karampai" yeṉṟār. karampai yeṉpuḻi ātaliṉ eṉa oru col varuvikka. vaḷam
   vīṅkirukkai, karampaiyātaliṉ, eṉa iyaiyum. naṉṉilaṅkaḷai yaḻittuk
   kaḻutai yērpūṭṭi veḷvarakum koḷḷum vittutal paṇṭaiyōr
   muṟai;"iruṅkaḷantōṟum, veḷ vāyk kaḻutaip pulliṉam pūṭṭi, veḷḷai varakum
   koḷḷum vittum, vaikaluḻava" (puṟam 392) eṉṟu cāṉṟōr kūṟutal kāṇka.
   ivvāṟu tām irukkum irukkaikaḷ karampaiyāy viṭutalāl, vēntar mutalāyiṉār
   cennel luṇaviṉṟi vaṟumaiyuṟṟu valikuṉṟiṉa reṉpār"cennel valci yaṟiyār"
   eṉṟum, eṉavē, avartam nāṭṭai iṉitāḷutal illai yeṉṟaṟku"nāṭuṭaṉāṭal
   yāvaṇatavarkkē" yeṉṟum kūṟiṉār."pāṭal cāṉṟa vaippiṉ nāṭu" eṉṟatu,
   nāṭṭiṉ naṉmai kūṟi yiraṅkiyatu.
   itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyatu: poṟaiya, nī arumpoṟi vayamāṉ aṉaiyai; vēntarum
   vēḷirum piṟarum kīḻppaṇintu niṉ vaḻippaṭārāyiṉ, avaruṭaiya vaḷam
   vīṅkirukkai, karampai yātaliṉ, cennel valci yaṟiyārāyt tattam
   nāṭuṭaṉāṭal avarkku yāvaṇatām eṉpatām. iṉip paḻaiyavuraikārar,
   vaṉpulantaḻīi yeṉṟu pāṭaṅkoṇṭu, ataṉaik karampaiyoṭu kūṭṭi, iruntu eṉa
   oru col varuvittu, vaṉpulam taḻīi yiruntu eṉṟum, meṉpāltōṟum iruntu
   eṉṟum, iyaittu, "poṟaiya, nī puli koṉṟu kaḷiṟaṭūum vayamāṉ aṉaiyai;
   ataṉāl vēntarum vēḷirum piṟarum niṉṉaṭikkīḻp paṇintu, tamakkuriya meṉ
   pālkaṭōṟum iruntu muṉ paṇintavāṟṟiṟ kēṟpa niṉvaḻi yoḻukārāyiṉ, avarkaḷ
   veḷvarakuḻuta koḷḷuṭaik karampaiyākiya vaṉpālilē keṭṭuppō yiruntu āṇṭu
   viḷainta veḷvaraku uṇpataṉṟit tām paṇṭuṇṇum cennel valci uṇṇak
   kiṭaiyātapaṭi miṭipaṭukiṉṟār; tattam nāṭṭiṉai oruṅku āḷutal avarkku
   yāvaṇatu eṉa viṉai muṭivu ceyka" eṉpar.
   "itaṉāṟ colliyatu avaṉ veṉṟicciṟappuk kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu"
   ----------------------------
                     ___________________________________

                          8.6. mā cita ṟirukkai.

   76
   kaḷiṟuṭaip peruñcaman tataiya veḵkuyart
       toḷiṟu vāṇmaṉṉar tutainilai koṉṟu
       muracu kaṭippaṭaiya varuntuṟaip pōkip
       peruṅkaṭa ṉīntiya maramvali yuṟukkum
       paṇṇiya vilaiñar pōlap puṇṇorīip         5
       peruṅkait toḻutiyiṉ vaṉṟuyar kaḻippi
       irantōr vāḻa nalki yirappōrk
       kīta ṟaṇṭā mācita ṟirukkai
       kaṇṭaṉeṉ celku vantaṉeṉ kālkoṇṭu
       karuvivāṉan taṇṭaḷi corinteṉap         10
       palvitai yuḻaviṟ cillē rāḷar
       paṉitturaip pakaṉṟaip pāṅkuṭait teriyal
       kaḻuvuṟu kaliṅkaṅ kaṭuppac cūṭi
       ilaṅku katirt tirumaṇi peṟūum
       akaṉkaṇ vaippi ṉāṭukiḻa vōyē.         15

   ituvumatu.
   peyar: mā cita ṟirukkai.
   9-15 kāl koṇṭu........nāṭukiḻa vōyē.
   urai; karuvi vāṉam kāl koṇṭu taṇtaḷi corinteṉa-tokuti koṇṭa maḻai mēkam
   nāṟṟicaiyum kāliṟaṅkit taṇṇiya maḻaiyaip peytatāka; pal vitai uḻaviṉ
   cil ērāḷar-mikutiyāka vitaittaṟ kēṟpap paranta uḻu nilaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya
   cilavākiya ērkaḷaiyuṭaiya uḻavar; paṉittuṟaip pakaṉṟaip pāṅkuṭait
   teriyal-kuḷirnta nīrt tuṟaiyiṭattē malarntuḷḷa pakaṉṟaip pūvāṟ ṟoṭutta
   aḻakuṭaiya mālaiyai; kaḻuvuṟu kaliṅkam kaṭuppac cūṭi-veḷukkappaṭṭa
   veḷḷāṭai pōlat talaiyiṟ cūṭikkoṇṭu; ilaṅku katirt tirumaṇi peṟūum-uḻum
   paṭaiccāliṭattē viḷaṅkukiṉṟa katirkaḷaiyuṭaiya aḻakiya maṇikaḷaip
   peṟukiṉṟa; akaṉkaṇ vaippiṉ nāṭu kiḻavōyakaṉṟa iṭamuṭaiya ūrkaḷ
   poruntiya nāṭṭukku uriyōṉē e-ṟu.
   iṭi, miṉṉal, maḻai mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ tokutiyātalpaṟṟi, "karuvi vāṉam"
   eṉappaṭṭatu. maḻai mukil nāṟṟicaiyum paravumiṭattuk kālviṭ ṭiṟaṅkutal
   kaṇkūṭātaliṉ"kālkoṇṭu" eṉṟār. kāl koṇṭeṉpataṟkup peytalait toṭaṅki
   yeṉṟalumām. vitaiyiṉ perumaiyai nōkka, uḻutaṟkuriya ērkaḷ
   ciṟiyavātalpaṟṟi, "palvitai yuḻaviṟ cillērāḷar" eṉṟār."akala
   vuḻuvatiṉum āḻa vuḻutal vēṇṭumātalāl ataṟkēṟṟa perumaiyuṭaiya vākātu
   ciṟumaiyuṭaimaipaṟṟiyē cillērāḷar" eṉṟu kūṟal vēṇṭiṟṟu. ciṉmai, ciṟumai
   kuṟittu niṉṟatu. paḻaiyavuraikārarum, "ciṉmaiyaic ciṉṉū leṉṟatupōla
   īṇṭuc ciṟumaiyākakkoḷka" eṉṟār. cillērāḷar uḻuta paṭaiccāl mika
   āḻamuṭaittaṉ ṟāyiṉum, ataṉkaṇṇum avarkaḷ mikka viḷaipayaṉē yaṉṟi
   uyariya maṇikaḷaip peṟukiṉṟārka ḷeṉpar, "cillērāḷar ilaṅkukatirt
   tirumaṇi peṟūum nāṭu" eṉṟār.
   pakaṉṟaimalarkku veḷutta āṭaiyai uvamai kūṟutalum, āṭaikku ammalarai
   yuvamai kūṟutalum cāṉṟōr vaḻakkākum."pōtu viri pakaṉṟaip putumala
   raṉṉa, akaṉṟumaṭi kaliṅka muṭīic celvamum, kēṭiṉṟi nalkumati peruma"
   (puṟam 393) eṉṟu nalliṟaiyaṉār eṉṉum cāṉṟōr kūṟutal kāṇka. pakaṉṟaiyaik
   kaṇṇiyākat toṭut taṇitalē perumpāṉmai vaḻakkātaliṉ, teriya leṉṟatu
   kaṇṇiyeṉak koḷḷappaṭṭatu;"pakaṉṟaik kaṇṇip pallāṉ kōvalar" (aiṅ 87)
   eṉṟum, "pakaṉṟaik kaṇṇip paḻaiyar" (malaipaṭu.459) eṉṟum varutal kāṇka.
   calavai ceyyappaṭṭa āṭai yeṉṟaṟkuk"kaḻuvuṟu kaliṅkam" eṉṟār. uyarnta
   maṇi yeṉpār"tirumaṇi" yeṉṟār. peru varuvāyuṭaimai tōṉṟa, akaṉkaṇ
   vaippiṉ nāṭu eṉṟu ciṟappikkappaṭṭatu.
   iṉip paḻaiyavuraikārar, "taṇṭaḷi corinteṉa ērāḷar katirt tirumaṇi
   peṟūum nāṭu eṉak kūṭṭi, maḻai peytalāṉē ērāḷar uḻutu viḷaittukkōṭalē
   yaṉṟi uḻuta iṭaṅkaḷtōṟum oḷiyaiyuṭaiya tirumaṇikaḷai yeṭuttuk koḷḷum
   nāṭeṉa vuraikka" eṉṟum"palvitai yuḻaviṉ cillērāḷa reṉṟatu pala vitai
   yuḻavāṟ periyārāyiṉum tam kulattāṉum oḻukkattāṉum ciṟiya ērāḷa
   reṉṟavā"ṟeṉṟum, "pakaṉṟait teriyal kaḻuvuṟu kaliṅkam kaṭuppac cūṭit
   tiru maṇi peṟūum eṉak kūṭṭi, pakaṉṟaimālaiyaik kaḻuvuṟu kaliṅkam oppac
   cūṭikkoṇṭu niṉṟu tirumaṇikaḷai yeṭukkumeṉa vuraikka" eṉṟum kūṟuvar.
   1-9 kaḷiṟuṭaip peruñ camam........vantaṉeṉ
   urai: kaḷiṟu uṭaip peruñcamam tataiya-kaḷiṟukaḷaik koṇṭu ceyyum periya
   pōr keṭumāṟu; eḵku uyarttu- vēlum vāḷum ēntic ceṉṟu; oḷiṟu vāḷ maṉṉar
   tutai nilai koṉṟu-viḷaṅkukiṉṟa vāḷaiyuṭaiya pakai maṉṉar tammiṟ
   kūṭiniṉṟu porum pōr nilaiyaik koṉṟaḻittu; muracu kaṭippu aṭaiya-veṟṟi
   muracai ataṉ kaṭippu aṟaintu muḻakka;
   aruntuṟai pōki-ceytaṟkariya pōrc ceyaṟkuriya tuṟai muṟṟavum taṭai pōkac
   ceṉṟu; peruṅkaṭal nīntiya maram valiyuṟukkum paṇṇiya vilaiñarpōla-peruṅ
   kaṭalaik kaṭantu ceṉṟu mīḷutalāl paḻutuṟṟa marakkalattiṉ paḻutu pōkkip
   paṇṭupōla valiyuṭaittākkum kaṭal vāṇikarpōla; peruṅkait toḻutiyiṉ
   puṇṇoi vaṉtuyar kaḻippi-periya kaiyaiyuṭaiya yāṉaikkūṭṭam uṟṟa
   pōrppuṇkaḷai āṟṟi avaṟṟāl avai tuṉpuṟṟa valiya tuyarattaiyum pōkki;
   irantōr vāḻa nalki-muṟpōtil vantu irantavar vaṟumai nīṅki vāḻumāṟu
   koṭuttu; irappōrkku-piṟpōtilvantu irap pōrukkum; ītal taṇṭāta-ītaliṉ
   kuṉṟāta; mā citaṟu irukkai-kutiraikaḷai varaiyātu vaḻaṅkum niṉ pācaṟai
   iruppiṉai; kaṇṭaṉeṉ celku vantaṉeṉ-kaṇṭupōvāṉ vantēṉ e-ṟu.
   kaḷiṟuṭaimai pōrkkup perumai tarutaliṉ, "kaḷiṟuṭaip peruñ camam"
   eṉṟār."yāṉai yuṭaiya paṭaikāṇṭal muṉṉiṉitē" (iṉi 40. 5) eṉṟu cāṉṟōr
   kūṟutal kāṇka. tataital, keṭutal;"vēluṭaik kuḻūuc caman tataiya nūṟi"
   (patiṟ 66) eṉap piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka. tutai nilai, kūṭiyirukkum
   aṇivakuppu; aṇi nilaiyai yaḻitta vaḻi, vīrar paṭaiyum piṟa paṭaikaḷum
   oḻuṅkiṉṟit tāṟumāṟāyc citaṟi eḷitil aḻiva rātaliṉ, "tutai nilai koṉṟu"
   eṉṟār. citait teṉṉātu"koṉ" ṟeṉṟār. maṟuvalum tutaintu nilaipeṟā
   vakaiyil aḻittamai tōṉṟa. muracu kaṭippu aṭaiya eṉṟatu, muracu muḻaṅka
   eṉṟavāṟu. pōrmuṟai veṭci mutalākap pal vakaippaṭutaliṉ, "aruntuṟai
   pōki" eṉṟār. peruṅkaṭalaip pakaivar paṭaikkum, marakkalattaik kaḷiṟṟut
   toḻutikkum, paṇṇiya vilaiñaraic cēramāṉ vīrarukkum uvamamākak koḷka.
   peruṅ kāṟṟum pēralaiyum mōtutalāl kaṭṭuttaḷarnta kalattai,
   karaicērntatum kaṭṭuṭait tākki valiyuṟuttal kalañ celuttuvōrkku
   iṉṟiyamaiyāc ceytiyākum. peruṅ kaṭal nīntiya marameṉṟatu marakkalattai.
   palvēṟu paṇṭaṅkaḷaiyum kalattiṟ koṇṭu vēṟu nāṭu ceṉṟu viṟṟup
   poruḷīṭṭutalpaṟṟik kalattiṟ cellum kaṭal vāṇikaraip"paṇṇiya vilai ñar"
   eṉṟār. yāṉaikaṭkup pōriluṇṭākiya puṇṇāl mikka tuyaruṇṭāvatai yaṟintu
   puṇṇai yāṟaṟu mukattāl tuyar pōkkutalāl, "puṇṇorīi vaṉṟuyar kaḻippi"
   yeṉṟār. vaṉṟuyar eṉṟataṉāl, uṟṟa puṇ perum puṇṇeṉ ṟaṟika. irantōr
   irappōr eṉa iṟappiṉum etirviṉum kūṟiyataṉāl muṟpoḻutu piṟpoḻutu koḷḷap
   paṭṭaṉa. muṟpoḻutu vantirantōr kaḷiṟu peṟṟuc ceṉṟamaiyiṉ, piṟpoḻutu
   varuvōrkkut kutiraikaḷai vaḻaṅkalāṉā ṉeṉa yuṇarka; eṇṇiṟantaṉa vātaliṉ,
   kutiraikaḷaic citaṟiṉāṉ eṉṟār; ivai pakaivariṭattē peṟṟaṉavām. iṉi,
   paḻaiyavuraikārarum, "mā citaṟirukkai yeṉṟatu pakaivariṭattuk
   koḷḷappaṭṭa mākkaḷai varaiyātu aḷaviṟakkak koṭukkum pācaṟai yirukkai
   yeṉṟavāṟu" eṉṟum"ic ciṟappāṉē itaṟku mācita ṟirukkai yeṉṟu peyarāyiṟ"
   ṟeṉṟum kūṟuvar.
   itukāṟum kūṟiyatu: vāṉam taḷi corinteṉac cillē rāḷar pakaṉṟait teriyal
   kaliṅkam kaṭuppac cūṭit tirumaṇi peṟūum akaṉkaṇ vaippiṉ nāṭu kiḻavōy,
   peruñ camam tataiya eḵkuyarttu, maṉṉar tutainilai koṉṟu, aruntuṟai
   pōki, paṇṇiya vilaiñarpōlap peruṅkait toḻutiyiṉ puṇṇorīi vaṉṟuyar
   kaḻippi, irantōr nalki, irappōrkku ītal taṇṭā mācita ṟirukkai kaṇṭaṉeṉ
   celku vantaṉeṉ eṉpatām. paḻaiyavuraikārar, "nāṭu kiḻavōy, maṉṉar peruñ
   camam tataiya eḵkuyarttu am maṉṉar palar kūṭic ceṟinta nilaimaiyaik
   koṉṟu aruntuṟai pōkik kaṭalai nīntiṉa marakkalattiṉai aḻivu cērātu
   valiyuṟukkum paṇṭavāṇikaraip pōlak kaittoḻutiyiṉ puṇṇai oruvuvittu
   valiya tuyaraik kaḻittup pōriṭattu viṉaiyiliruttalē vinōtamākak koṇṭu
   irantōrvāḻa nalkip piṉṉum irappōrkku ītaliṉ māṟāta mā citaṟirukkaiyaik
   kaṇṭu pōvēṉ vantēṉ eṉak kūṭṭi viṉai muṭivu ceyka" eṉpar.
   "itaṉāṟ colliyatu avaṉ veṉṟicciṟappum koṭaic ciṟappum kūṟiyavāṟām"

                      8.7. veṉṟāṭu tuṇaṅkai.

   77
   eṉaipperum paṭaiyaṉō ciṉappōrp poṟaiyaṉ
       eṉṟaṉi rāyi ṉāṟucel vampalir
       maṉpatai peyara varacukaḷat toḻiyak
       koṉṟutō ḷōcciya veṉṟāṭu tuṇaṅkai
       mīpiṇat turuṇṭa tēyā vāḻiyiṟ         5
       paṇṇamai tēru māvu mākkaḷum
       eṇṇaṟ karumaiyi ṉeṇṇiṇṟō vilavē
       kantukō ḷīyātu kāḻpala murukki
       ukakakum parunti ṉilattuniḻal cāṭic
       cēṇparaṉ murampi ṉīrmpaṭaik koṅkar         10
       āparan taṉṉapal celaviṉ
       yāṉaikāṇ palavaṉ ṟāṉai yāṉē.

   tuṟai: uḻiñai yaravam
   vaṇṇamum tūkkum atu
   peyar: veṉṟāṭu tuṇaṅkai.
   1-2. eṉaip perum paṭaiyaṉō................vampalir
   urai: āṟu cel vampalir-ivvaḻiyē cellum putuvōrkaḷē; ciṉap pōrp
   poṟaiṉ-ciṉattuṭaṉ pōrai veṟṟiyuṇṭākac ceyyum peruñ cēralirum poṟai;
   eṉaipperum paṭaiyaṉō eṉṟaṉirāyiṉ-ettuṇaipperiya paṭaiyaiyuṭaiyavaṉō
   eṉṟu kēṭkiṉṟīrāyiṉ, kūṟuval kēṇmiṉ e-ṟu.
   vampalar, putuvēr."ārum aruvum īroṭu civaṇuma" (col viḷi. 21)
   eṉṟataṉāl, vampalir eṉa vantatu. irantōrkku eṇṇiṟanta kaḷiṟukaḷaiyum
   irappōrkku aḷaviṟanta mākkaḷaiyum citaṟutalaik kāṇ pōrkkum,
   cellumiṭantōṟum veṟṟiyē peṟak kāṇpōrkkum poṟaiyaṉatu paṭaip perumai
   yitaṟku vēṭkai nikaḻu mātaliṉ, "eṉaip perum paṭaiyaṉō eṉṟaṉirāyiṉ"
   eṉṟār. eṉaip perum paṭaiyaṉō eṉṟatu vampalar kūṟṟiṉaikkoṇṭu kūṟiyatu.
   ciṉattiṉ viḷaivu pōrum, ap pōriṉ viḷaivu veṉṟiyu mātaliṉ, "ciṉap pōrp
   poṟaiyaṉ" eṉṟu ciṟappittār. kūṟuval kēṇmiṉ eṉpatu eñci niṉṟatu.
   3-7.
   maṉpatai........cilaṉē
   urai: maṉpatai peyara-pakaippaṭaiyiluḷḷa vīrar aḻintōṭavum; aracu kaḷat
   toḻiya-pakaiyaracar pōrkkaḷattē paṭṭu viḻavum; koṉṟu-appakaivaraik
   koṉṟu; tōḷōcciya veṉṟāṭu tuṇaṅkai-tōḷai yuyarttik kaivīciyāṭiya veṉṟāṭu
   tuṇaṅkaiyiṉai yuṭaiyarāyp paṭṭu vīḻnta; mīpiṇattu-avar piṇattiṉmītu;
   uruṇṭa tēyā āḻiyiṉ- uruṇṭōṭiya vāy tēyāta cakkarattaiyuṭaiya; paṇṇamai
   tērum-kaṭukic cellutaṟ kēṟpap paṇṇutalamainta tērkaḷum; māvum
   mākkaḷum-kutiraikaḷum kālāṭkaḷum; eṇṇaṟ karumaiyiṉ-eṇṇa muṭiyāta
   aḷavilamaintiruttalāl; eṇṇiṉṟō vilaṉ-eṇṇiṟṟilēṉ e-ṟu
   pakaivar paṭaivīrarai maṉpatai (Mob) yeṉṟār; vīrar eṉappaṭaṟkuriya
   aḻiyāmai avarpāl iṉmaiyiṉ. uyir nīṅkiyavaḻi araca pōkamum piṟavum
   oḻitaliṉ, "aracu kaḷattoḻiya" veṉṟār. vīrar pōril pakaivarai veṉṟu
   appōrkkaḷattē kaiyai vīcit tuṇaṅkaiyāṭit tamveṉṟi makiḻcciyāl iṉpuṟupa
   vātaliṉ , ak kūttiṉai"veṉṟāṭu tuṇaṅkai" yeṉṟār. paḻaiyavuraikārar,
   "veṉṟāṭu tuṇaṅkaip piṇam" eṉṟatu ūrkaḷilē yāṭum tuṇaṅkai yaṉṟik
   kaḷaṅkaḷilē veṉṟāṭiṉa tuṇaṅkaiyaiyuṭaiya piṇam eṉṟavā" ṟeṉṟum, "ic
   ciṟappāṉē itaṟku veṉṟāṭu tuṇaṅkai yeṉṟu peyarāyiṟṟeṉṟum kūṟuvar. aracu
   kaḷattoḻiya veṉṟataṟkup paḻaiyavuraikārar"aracaik kaḷattilē uṭaloḻintu
   kiṭakkak koṉṟavā" ṟeṉṟum, "koṉṟu tōḷōc;ciya piṇam eṉak kūṭṭi, muṉpu
   tammuṭaṉ pakaitta varaik koṉṟu tōḷōcci yāṭi ippoḻutu ivaṉ kaḷattiṟ
   paṭṭuk kiṭakkiṉṟa vīrar piṇameṉa av vīrar ceytiyai ap piṇattiṉ mēlēṟṟic
   colliyavāṟāka vuraikka" eṉṟum, "mī piṇattaip piṇamīyeṉak koḷka" eṉṟum
   kūṟuvar. eṇṇiṟṟō veṉaṟpālatu melintu niṉṟatu.
   8-12. kantu..................tāṉaiyāṉē.
   urai: kan;tu kōḷīyātu-kaṭṭut taṟiyōṭu piṇippuṇṭaṟku iṭantarātu; kāḻpala
   murukki-kuttukkōla palavaṟ ṟaiyum citaittu; ukakkum paruntiṉ nilattu
   niḻal cāṭi- uyarntu paṟakkum paruntiṉatu nilattiṭattē vīḻum niḻalaic
   ciṉantu pāyum;cēṇparal murampiṉ-cēṇiṭameṅkum paranta parukkaik
   kaṟkaḷaiyuṭaiya murampu niltilē; īrmpaṭaik koṅkar ā parantaṉṉa-īriya
   paṭaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya koṅkaruṭaiya āṉiraikaḷ parantu celvatupōṉṟa; celaviṉ
   palyāṉai-cela viṉaiyuṭaiya palavākiya yāṉaikaḷai; avaṉ
   tāṉaiyāṉ-avaṉuṭaiya tāṉaiyilē; kāṇpal-kāṇkiṉṟēṉēyaṉṟi avai ittuṇai
   yeṉṟu aṟintilēṉ e-ṟu
   kantiṉiṭattē piṇittaṟku aṭaṅkātu ak kantiṉai muṟit taḻittal paṟṟi,
   "kantu kōḷīyātu" eṉṟum kuttappaṭum kuttukkōl av yāṉaikaḷiṉ uṭaliṟ paṭṭu
   ūṭuṟuvum valiyiṉmaiyiṉ citaintu pōvatu tōṉṟa, " kāḻpala murukki"
   yeṉṟum, poruḷallāta niḻalaiyum pakaip poruḷākak karutic ciṉavutaṟ kēṟṟa
   matak kaḷippuṭaimaipaṟṟi"ukakkum paruntiṉ nilattu niḻal cāṭi" yeṉṟum
   kūṟiṉār. piṇip puṇṇā teṉṉum poruṭṭāya"kōḷīyātu" eṉṉum etirmaṟai
   viṉaiyec cattiricol murukki, cāṭi yeṉṉum ecca viṉaikaḷuṭaṉ aṭukki
   niṉṟu"celaviṉ" eṉpataik koṇṭu muṭintaṉa. ukattal, uyartal.
   kaṇṇuk keṭṭiya vaḷavum pāl niṟainta murampu nilamē kāṇap paṭutal paṟṟi,
   "cēṇ paral murampu" eṉṟum nīr vēṇ;ṭik kūval mutaliyaṉa tōṇṭutaṟkuriya
   kuntāli mutaliya paṭaikaḷai"īrmpaṭai" yeṉṟum kūṟiṉār. koṅkar ākkaḷ
   palavuṭaiya reṉpatai"ā keḻu koṅkar" (patiṟ 22) eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟutal
   kāṇka. yāṉaik kūṭ ṭattiṉ mikutiyiṉai āḷiraik kūṭṭattai yuvamaṅ kāṭṭi
   yuraittal marapu;"erumaiyaṉṉa karuṅkal liṭaitō, ṟāṉiṟ pākkum yāṉaiya"
   (puṟam 5) eṉṟu cāṉṟōr uraippatu kāṇka.
   "pal yāṉai kāṇpal" eṉṟataṉāl avaṟṟai ittuṇaiyeṉa yeṇṇaṟ karumai
   peṟappaṭṭatu.
   itukāṟum kūṟiyatu: āṟu cel vampalir, ciṉap pōrp poṟaiyaṉ ēṉaip perum
   paṭaiyaṉō eṉṟaṉirāyiṉ, avaṉuṭaiya tērum mākkaḷum eṇṇaṟ karumaiyiṉ
   eṇṇiṉṟō vilaṉ; avaṉ tāṉaiyilē koṅkar āparanaṉṉa celaviṉ pal yāṉai
   kāṇpal; avaṟṟaiyum ittuṇaiya veṉṟu eṇṇiṟṟilēṉ eṉpatām. iṉi
   paḻaiyavuraikārar, "vampalir, poṟaiyaṉ eṉaip perum paṭaiyaṉ
   eṉaṟaṉirāyiṉ avaṉ tāṉaiyiṭattut tērum māvum mākkaḷum eṇṇaṟ karumaiyiṉ
   eṇṇiṟṟilaṉ; āyiṉ, avaṉ tāṉaiyiṉ yāṉai tāṉ eṇṇiṉaiyō veṉiṉ atuvum
   eṇṇiṉēṉallēṉ; kaṭpulaṉukku varaiyaṟaippaṭṭatupōla āparantālotta celaviṟ
   pala yāṉaiyai avaṉ tāṉaiyāṉē kāṇpal eṉak kūṭṭi viṉai muṭivu ceyka, "
   eṉpar
   "itaṉāṟ colliyatu avaṉ paṭaip perumaic ciṟappuk kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu"
   "ippāṭṭiṟ potuppaṭap paṭaiyeḻucci kūṟiyataṉai uḻiñaiyarava meṉṟatu,
   āṇṭu appaṭai yeḻuṅ kālattu nocci mītiṟ pōr kuṟitteḻuntatai oru
   kāraṇattāl aṟintupōlum, " eṉṟu paḻaiyavurai kūṟiyatu ārāyattakkatu.

                        8.8. piṟaḻa nōkkiyavar.

   78
   valampaṭu muraci ṉilaṅkuvaṉa viḻūum
       avveḷ ḷaruvi yuvvarai yatuvē
       cilvaḷai viṟali celkuvai yāyiṉ
       vaḷḷaḷitaḻt tāmarai neytalo ṭarintu
       melliyaṉ makaḷi rolkuvaṉa riyalik         5
       kiḷikaṭi mēvalar puṟavutoṟu nuvalap
       palpaya ṉilaiiya kaṭaṟuṭai vaippiṉ
       velpō rāṭavar maṟampurintu kākkum
       vilpayi liṟumpiṟ ṟakaṭūr nūṟi
       pēema maṉṟa piṟaḻa nōkkiyavar         10
       ōṭuṟu kaṭumuraṇ ṭumiyac ceṉṟu
       vemmuṉai taputta kālait tannāṭ
       ṭiyāṭuparan taṉṉa māviṉ
       āparan taṉṉa yāṉaiyōṉ kuṉṟē.

   tuṟai: viṟaliyāṟṟuppaṭai
   peyar: piṟaḻa nōkkiyavar.
   3 cilvaḷai............yāyiṉ
   urai: cilvaḷaiviṟali-cilavākiya vaḷaikaḷai yaṇinta viṟaliyē;
   celakuvaiyāyiṉ-peruñcēral irumpoṟaipāl cella virumpiṉaiyāyiṉ.
   viṟaliyaic cēramāṉiṭattē āṟṟuppaṭukkiṉṟārātaliṉ, cilvaḷai viṟaliyeṉac
   ciṟappittār. viṟalpaṭap pāṭiyāṭu makaḷātaliṉ, āṭumiṭattu vaḷaipalavāya
   vaḻi oṉṟiṉoṉṟu tākkiyuṭaiyumātalāl cilavēyaṇital avaṭku iyalpu eṉa
   aṟika; iṉi, palvaḷaiyiṭum paruvattāḷallaḷeṉpatu tōṉṟa ivvāṟu
   kūṟiṉāreṉṟumām. cēramāṉpāl cellum karuttuṭaiyaḷātalaic collālum
   kuṟippālum terivittāḷātalāl, "celkuvaiyāyiṉ" eṉṟār.
   4-14 vaḷḷitaḻ..................kuṉṟē.
   urai: melliyal makaḷir olkuvaṉar iyali-melliya iyalpiṉaiyuṭaiya makaḷir
   acaintu naṭantu maruta vayaṟkuc ceṉṟu; vaḷitaḻt tāmarai neytaloṭu
   arintu-vaḷaviya itaḻkaḷaiyuṭaiya tāmaraip pūvaiyum neytaṟ pūvaiyum
   koytu koṇṭu; puṟavutoṟum kiḷi kaṭi mēvalar; nuvala-mullaip pulattukkuc
   ceṉṟu āṇṭuḷḷa puṉantōṟum vantu paṭiyum kiḷikaḷai yōppum
   viruppuṭaiyarāyk kiḷi kaṭi pāṭṭaippāṭa; palpayaṉ nilaiiya kaṭaṟuṭai
   vaippiṉ-palvakaip payaṉkaḷum nilaipeṟṟa kāṭṭiṭattu ūrkaḷaiyum; velpōr
   āṭavar maṟam purintu kākkum vil payil iṟumpiṉ-vellukiṉṟa pōraiyuṭaiya
   vīrar maṟamē virumpik kāttaṟ ṟoḻilaic ceyyum viṟpaṭai nirampiya kāvaṟ
   kāṭṭaiyumuṭaiya; takaṭūr nūṟi - takaṭūrai yaḻittu; pēem amaṉṟa piṟaḻa
   nōkku iyavar-kaṇṭārkku accattai yuṇṭupaṇṇum pakaivaraip piṟaḻntu nōkkum
   pārvaiyiṉaiyum pala iyaṅkaḷaiyumuṭaiya pakai vīraruṭaiya; ōṭuṟu
   kaṭumuraṇ tumiyacceṉṟu-tammōṭu etirttār tōṟṟōṭutaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa vali
   keṭumāṟu mēṟceṉṟu; vemmuṉai taputta kālai-avaratu koṭiya pōr muṉaiyaip
   porutaḻitta kālattu; tamnāṭṭu yāṭu parantaṉṉamāviṉ-ap pakaivar nāṭṭilē
   āṭukaḷ parantāṟpōlap parantu tōṉṟum kutiraikaḷaiyum; ā parantaṉṉa
   yāṉaiyōṉ kuṉṟu-ākkaḷ parantāṟ pōlap parantu tōṉṟum yāṉaikaḷaiyu
   muṭaiyaṉākiya cēramāṉatu kuṉṟu;
   iyalpākavē meṉmaittaṉmaiyum ataṉāl acainta naṭaiyu muṭaiyavarātaliṉ
   makaḷirai, "melliyal makaḷir olkuvaṉa riyali" yeṉṟār. tāmaraiyum
   neytalum arintamai kūṟiyataṉāl, marutavayal peṟappaṭṭatu. oṭu, eṇṇoṭu.
   aṇmaiyilē mullaippuṟavu miruttaliṉ, marutanilañ ceṉṟa makaḷir uṭaṉē
   mullaippuṟavu cēṟalaiyum kūṟiṉār. puṟavu cēṟaṟkuk kāraṇam iḵteṉpār,
   "kiḷikaṭi mēvalar" eṉṟār. mullai mutaliya nāṉilappayaṉum oruṅkupeṟumāṟu
   tōṉṟa, palpayaṉ nilaiiya kaṭaṟuṭai vaippiṉ" eṉṟār. iṉip
   paḻaiyavuraikārar, "makaḷir iyali neytaloṭu tāmarai yarintu kiḷikaṭi
   mēvalar puṟavutoṟum nuvalap palpayaṉ nilaiiya kaṭaṟu eṉak kūṭṭik
   kiḷikaṭi makaḷir nilavaṇumai yāṉē marutanilattilē ceṉṟu neytaloṭu
   tāmarai yarintu, piṉ kiḷikaṭi toḻilai mēvutalaiyuṭaiyarāyp puṟaviṉ
   puṉaṅkaṭōṟum kiḷikaṭi pāṭalai nuvalap palpayaṅkaḷum nilaipeṟṟa mullai
   nilameṉa vuraikka" eṉṟār.
   vaippiṉaiyum iṟumpiṉaiyumuṭaiya takaṭūreṉa iyaiyum."vaippiṉ takaṭūr
   eṉak kūṭṭuka" eṉṟum, "āṭavar kākkum iṟuppeṉak kūṭṭuka" veṉṟum
   paḻaiyavuraikārar kūṟuvar. vilpayil iṟumpu, villēntiya vīrarpaṭai
   yirukkum iṟumpu. pēem, accam. kaṇṭārkku accattai yuṇṭupaṇṇum
   pārvaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya vīrar. pakaivarai nērē nōkkātu eṭuttum paṭuttum
   kōṭṭiyum pārkkum iyalpupaṟṟi, "piṟaḻa nōkkiyava"reṉṟār
   ;paḻaiyavuraikārarum"piṟaḻa nōkkiyava reṉṟatu, tam ciṉamikutiyāṉē
   māṟṟār paṭait tōṟṟattiṉai neṟiyāl nōkkātu eṭuttum paṭuttum kōṭṭiyum
   palapaṭap piṟaḻa nōkkum pakaivarākiya palliya muṭaiyāreṉṟavā, " ṟeṉṟum,
   "ic ciṟappāṉē itaṟkup piṟaḻa nōkkiyavareṉṟu peyarāyiṟ"ṟeṉṟum kūṟuvar.
   neṟiyāl nōkkiya vaḻi, paṭaiyiluḷḷāriṟ palar iṉiyarum neruṅkiya
   muṟaiyiṉarumāyk kāṇappaṭuvareṉṟum, akkālai yavarpāl kaṇṇōṭumāyiṉ maṟam
   vāṭumeṉṟum karutip piṟaḻa nōkkutu vīrarkku iyalpākaliṉālum appiṟaḻcci
   nōkkiṟku āṇṭu iyampum iyaṅkaḷ tuṇaiyākaliṉālum paṭaivīraraip"piṟaḻa
   nōkkiyavar" eṉṟār. ic ciṟappālē it toṭar, ippāṭṭiṟkup peyarāyiṟṟeṉak
   kōṭal cīriteṉa vaṟika. piṟaḻcci nōkkam kaṇṭārkku accam payakkum
   taṉmaikaḷ niṟaintiruttal paṟṟip"pēem amaṉṟa" eṉṟāreṉka. iṉi,
   ivviyavaratu valinilai kūṟuvar"ōṭuṟu kaṭumuraṇ" eṉṟār. ivviyavar
   takaṭūraik kāttuniṉṟa pakaivīrar. ap pakaivar koṅkarātalāl, avarpāl
   uḷḷa yāṭukaḷiṉ paṉmaiyum ākkaḷiṉ paṉmaiyum uṇarttuvār, "tannāṭṭu yāṭu
   parantaṉṉa māviṉ" eṉṟum, "āparantaṉṉa yāṉai"yeṉṟum kūṟiṉār.
   paḻaiyavuraikārar"māviṉoṭu veṉa oṭuvirittumuṉai taputtakālai māviṉoṭu ā
   parantaṉa yāṉaiyōṉ eṉa viṉaimuṭivu ceyka" eṉṟār.
   1-2. vavalampaṭu...........yatuvē.
   urai: uvvarai - uvvellaiyil uḷḷa; valampaṭu muraciṉ - veṟṟiyiṭattu
   muḻaṅkum muracupōla; ilaṅkuvaṉa viḻūum av veḷḷaruvi atu - muḻakkamum
   viḷakkamu muṭaiyavāy vīḻkiṉṟa aḻakiya veḷḷiya aruvikaḷaiyuṭaiya
   atuvākum e-ṟu
   varai, ellai. tōṉṟukiṉṟa kuṉṟukaḷil uyarntu aruvikaḷai yuṭaittāyt
   tōṉṟum kuṉṟiṉai"atu" eṉac cuṭṭik kāṭṭukiṉṟā rātaliṉ, "uvvarai av
   veḷḷaruvi yatuvē" eṉṟār. perumuḻakkam eḻutaliṉ, veṟṟi muraciṉai yuvamam
   kūṟiṉār."valampaṭu muracu" eṉavē veṟṟi muracātal peṟṟām. viḷakkam
   kūṟavē muḻakka muṇmai peṟappaṭṭatu,
   itukāṟum kūṟiyavāṟṟāl, "cilvaḷai viṟali, celkuvaiyāyiṉ yāṉaiyōṉ kuṉṟu
   uvvellaiyil veḷḷaruviyaiyuṭaiya atu eṉa māṟik kūṭṭi viṉaimuṭivu ceyka"
   itaṉāṟ colliyatu avaṉ veṉṟic ciṟappuk kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu.

                         8.9. niṟampaṭu kuruti.

   79
   uyirpōṟ ṟalaiyē ceruvat tāṉē
       koṭai pōṟ ṟalaiyē yiravalar naṭuvaṇ
       periyōrp pēṇic ciṟiyōrai yaḷitti
       niṉvayiṟ pirinta nallicai kaṉaviṉum
       piṟar nacai yaṟiyā vayaṅkucen nāviṉ         5
       paṭiyōrt tēytta vāṇmait toṭiyōr
       tōḷiṭaik kuḻainta kōtai mārpa
       aṉaiya vaḷapparuṅ kuraiyai yataṉāl
       niṉṉoṭu vārār tannilat toḻintu
       kolkaḷiṟ ṟiyāṉai yeruttam pulleṉa         10
       vilkulai yaṟuttuk kōliṉ vārā
       velpōr vēntar muracukaṇ pōḻntavar
       aracuvā vaḻaippak kōṭaṟut tiyaṟṟiya
       aṇaṅkuṭai marapiṟ kaṭṭiṉmē liruntu
       tumpai cāṉṟa meytayaṅ kuyakkattu         15
       niṟampaṭu kuruti puṟampaṭi ṉallatu
       maṭai yetir koḷḷā vañcuvaru marapiṟ
       kaṭavu ḷayiraiyi ṉilaiik
       kēṭila vāka perumaniṉ pukaḻē.

   tuṟai: centuṟaip pāṭāṇpāṭṭu
   vaṇṇamum tūkkum atu.
   peyar: niṟampaṭu kuruti.
   1-8. uyir.............kuraiyai
   urai: vayaṅku cen nāviṉ - meymmai moḻiyāl viḷakkam poruntiya cevviya
   nāviṉaiyum; paṭiyōr tēytta āṇmai - vaṇaṅkātārai valiyaḻitta
   āṇmaiyiṉaiyum; toṭi yōr tōḷiṭaik kuḻaitta kōtai mārpa - toṭiyaṇinta
   makaḷir tōḷaik kūṭutalāl kuḻainta mālai yaṇinta mārpaiyumuṭaiya vēntē;
   ceruvattāṉ uyir pōṟṟalai - pōrilē nī uyiraip poruḷākak karutiṟṟilai;
   iravalar naṭuvaṇ koṭai pōṟṟalai - paricil vēṇṭivarum irappōr kūṭṭattilē
   koṭukkuñ ceyalil etaṉaiyum eṉṟum varaitalai yaṟiyāy; periyōrp pēṇic
   ciṟiyōrai aḷitti - periyōrkaḷait tamarākap pēṇikkoṇṭu āṟṟalāṟ
   ciṟiyarāyiṉāraiyum puṟak kaṇiyātu aruḷ ceykiṉṟāy; pirinta niṉ vayiṉ
   nallicai- ellāt ticaiyiṉum ceṉṟu paraviyirukkum niṉ nalla pukaḻkaḷ;
   kaṉaviṉum piṟar nacai yaṟiyā - kaṉavilum tammai virumpum piṟarai
   virumpic cellāvāyiṉa; aṉaiya aḷapparuṅ kuraiyai - at taṉmaiyavākiya
   aḷattaṟkuriya kuṇañ ceyalkaḷai yuṭaiyaiyā yirukkiṉṟāy. e--ṟu.
   nāviṟku viḷakkam, tāṉ vaḻaṅkum meymmai moḻiyālum, cemmai, yātoṉṟum
   tīmaiyilāta collutalālumātaliṉ"vayaṅku cennāviṉ" eṉṟār. paṭiyār,
   paṭiyōreṉa niṉṟatu; paṭiyār, vaṇaṅkātār;"paṭiyōrt tēytta paṇivilāṇmai*"
   (malaipaṭu. 423) eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka. paṭital,
   vaṇaṅkutal."paṭiyō reṉṟatu piratiyōreṉṉum vaṭamoḻittirivu" (akam. 22)
   eṉa aka nāṉūṟṟu arumpatavuraikārar kūṟiyatu coṉṉilai yuṇarātu
   kūṟiyatākaliṉ poruntāmai yaṟika. makaḷir muyakkiṭai mārpilaṇinta tāru
   mālaiyuṅ kuḻaiyumātaliṉ" toṭiyōr tōḷiṭaik kuḻainta kōtai mārpa"
   eṉṟār;"kātal koḷḷāp palliruṅ kūntal makaḷir ollāmuyakkiṭaik kuḻaika
   veṉtārē" (puṟam-73) eṉac cōḻaṉ nalaṅkiḷḷi kūṟṟālum ītaṟiyappaṭum
   ceru, ammuppeṟṟuc ceruvameṉa vantatu. ceruviṉkaṇ piṟakkum pukaḻmēṟ
   ceṉṟa vēṭkaiyāl uyiraip poruḷākak karutiṟṟilaṉātalāl, " uyir pōṟṟalaiyē
   ceruvattāṉē" eṉṟār.
   -----------
   * maṟavēntarkkup paṭimaiyāvatu **ōṭṭāta pakaivarai vaṇaṅkip
   piṉṉillāmai; ataṉaiyuṭaiya vēntar paṭiyōr eṉappaṭṭaṉareṉa aṟika.
   "cuḻalumicai vēṇṭi vēṇṭā vuyirār;" (kuṟaḷ 777) eṉac cāṉṟōr kūṟutal
   kāṇka. īttuvakkum iṉpattālum īkaikkaṇ icai niṟpatālum varaiyātu
   vaḻaṅkum vaṇmaiyuṭaiyaṉātal kaṇṭu"koṭai pōṟṟalaiyē iravalar naṭuvaṇ"
   eṉṟār. koṭai pōṟṟāmaiyāvatu "iṉṟu celiṉun tarumē ciṟuvarai. niṉṟu
   celiṉuntarumē piṉṉum, muṉṉē tantaṉe ṉeṉṉātu tuṉṉi, vaikalum celiṉum
   poyyalaṉāki" (puṟam.171) irappōr vēṇṭiya vēṇṭiyāṅku vaḻaṅkuvatu.
   periyāraip pēṇikkoḷal aracarkkup peruvaṉmaiyātalāl, "periyōrppaṇi"
   yeṉṟār.;"tammiṟ periyār tamarā voḻukutal vaṉmaiyu ḷellān talai" (kuṟaḷ
   444) eṉac cāṉṟōrum paṇittār. ciṟiyōreṉṟatu aṟivu, āṇmai, poruḷ, paṭai
   mutaliyavaṟṟāl taṉṉiṟ ciṟumaiyuṭaiyārai. avarai yaḷittōmpalum ceṅkōṉmai
   yātaliṉ, "ciṟiyōrai yaḷitti" yeṉṟār;"vallārāyiṉum vallunarāyiṉum,
   varunti vantōr maruṅku nōkki, aruḷavallaiyākumati" (puṟam 27) eṉac
   cāṉṟōr kūṟum mutumoḻik kāñciyāṉu maṟika. niṉvayiṉ nallicai, pirinta
   nallicaiyeṉa iyaiyum. pukaḻvarum vāyilkaḷ kalvi, āṇmai mutalākap
   palavātaliṉ, pukaḻum palavātalpaṟṟi "nallicai yaṟiyā" eṉap paṉmaiyāṟ
   kūṟiṉār.
   oruvaṉ pukaḻkku avaṉ kāraṇamāyiṉum, avaṉait taṉakku ātāramākakkoḷḷātu
   avaṉiṟ pirintuceṉṟu ulakattai ātāramākakkoṇṭu avaṉ maṭiyiṉum tāṉ
   maṭiyātu niṉṟu nilavuvatu pukaḻk kiyalpātaliṉ, "niṉvayiṟ pirinta
   nallicai" yeṉṟār. pukaḻ taṉṉaic ceytōṉiṟ piriyātu avaṉōṭē kiṭakkumāyiṉ,
   avaṉ poṉṟuṅkāṟ ṟāṉum poṉṟu meṉpatupaṭṭuk kuṟṟamāy muṭitaliṉ, "pirinta
   nallicai" eṉac ciṟappittār. ik karuttēpaṟṟip
   paḻaiyavuraikārarum"pirinta niṉ vayiṉ nallicai yeṉak kūṭṭuka"
   eṉṟum"pirital taṉṉaiviṭṭut tikku vitikkukaḷilē pōtal" eṉṟum kūṟutal
   kāṇka."niṉ vayiṟ pirinta nallicai"yeṉak kiṭantapaṭiyē koṇṭu,
   "niṉpāṉiṉṟum pirintu ceṉṟa niṉatu nallicaikaḷ" eṉpāru muḷar;
   niṉpāṉiṉṟum pirintu ceṉṟa eṉṟa vaḻi niṉṉiṉ nīṅkiya pukaḻeṉṟākip poruḷ
   ciṟavā toḻitaliṉ, atu poruḷaṉmai yaṟika. nī peṟṟuḷḷa pukaḻkaḷaip piṟar
   kaṉaviṉum peṟṟaṟiyār eṉpār, pukaḻ mēlēṟṟi niṉ nallicai"kaṉaviṉum piṟar
   nacai yaṟiyā" eṉṟār."nacai yaṟiyā" yeṉṟataṉāl niṉpakaimaikkañcip piṟar
   kaṉaviṉum nī peṟṟa pukaḻkaḷaip peṟutaṟku virumpuvatilar eṉpatu
   kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu.
   ivvāṟu uyir pōṟṟāmaiyāl āṇmaiyum., koṭai pōṟṟāmaiyāl vaṇmaiyum,
   periyōrp pēṇic ciṟiyōrai yaḷittalāl ceṅkōṉmaiyum piṟavum
   aḷattaṟkariyavā yiruttalāl, ēṉaiya piṟavum attaṉmaiyavē yeṉpār, "aṉaiya
   vaḷapparuṅ kuraiyai" yeṉṟār.
   8-19. ataṉāl..........niṉ pukaḻē.
   urai: ataṉāl-ātalāl; niṉṉoṭu vārār-niṉ viruppappaṭi yoḻukutaṟ
   kicaiyāmal; tam nilattu oḻintu- niṉakku māṟupaṭṭut taṅkaḷ nāṭṭilēyē
   yiruntu; kolkaḷiṟṟu yāṉai eruttam pulleṉa vilkulai yaṟuttu-niṉṉai
   yetirtta piṟa vēntar tām ēṟivanta kollukiṉṟa kaḷiṟṟi yāṉaiyiṉ piṭari
   pulleṉṉumāṟu avar ēṟṟup poruta villiṉ nāṇai yaṟuttu avaraik koṉṟu nī
   veṟṟi mēmpaṭak kaṇṭum; kōliṉ vārā vel pōr vēntar-niṉ ceṅkōṟ kīḻp
   paṇintu varutalaic ceyyātu velkiṉṟa pōr ceytalaiyuṭaiya vēntaratu;
   muracukaṇ pōḻntu-muḻakkum muraciṉ kaṇṇaik kiḻittu; avar aracuvā
   aḻaippa-avarkaḷuṭaiya paṭṭatti yāṉai kataṟak kataṟa; kōṭuaṟuttu
   iyaṟṟiya aṇaṅkuṭaimarapiṉ kaṭṭiṉmēl iruntu-ataṉ kōṭṭiṉai yaṟuttuc ceyta
   teyvattaṉmai poruntiya muṟaimaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya kaṭṭiliṉ mēliruntu, ;
   tumpai cāṉṟa mey tayaṅku uyakkattu-tumpai cūṭip porutalil amainta
   meyyiṭattē yuṇṭākiya acaivupaṟṟip piṟnta ōyccaluṭaṉ; niṟampaṭu kuruti
   puṟam paṭiṉ allatu-mārpiṟ paṭṭa puṇṇiṭat toḻukum kurutiyāṟ puṟattē
   teḷikkap paṭṭālaṉṟi; maṭai etirkoḷḷā- koṭukkappaṭum paṭaiccalai
   (paliyai) yēṟṟuk koḷḷāta; añcu varumarapiṉ-accamporuntiya
   muṟaimaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya; kaṭavuḷ ayiraiyiṉ-koṟṟavai vīṟṟirukkiṉṟa ayirai
   malaipōla; peruma-perumāṉē; niṉ pukaḻnilaiik kēṭilavāka-niṉ pukaḻkaḷum
   nilaipeṟṟuk keṭātu viḷaṅkuvaṉavāka e-ṟu.
   ataṉāl eṉpataṉai nilaiik kēṭilavāka eṉpataṉōṭu iyaikka. tam maṉattuḷḷa
   māṟupāṭṭāl niṉ viruppuvaḻi yoḻukutaṟku icaintila reṉpār"vārār" eṉṟār.
   vārātu irunta nilai iḵteṉṟaṟkut"tannilan toḻintu" eṉṟār. oḻinteṉavē,
   avarpāl ceyalaṟuti peṟappaṭṭatu. iṉip paḻaiyavuraikārar"niṉṉoṭu vārār
   tannilat toḻinteṉṟatu niṉṉai vaḻipaṭṭu niṉṉōṭu oḻukā tiruttalē yaṉṟit
   tam nilattilē vēṟupaṭṭu niṉṟeṉṟavā" ṟeṉpar.
   niṉ viruppuvaḻi yoḻukutaṟku icaiviṉṟit tam nilattē vaṟi tiruttalum
   māṉamātaliṉ pōrkku varalāṉār; atu ceyyātu paṇintu niṉ kōṟkīḻ varaṟpālar
   eṉpār, "kōliṉ vārā" eṉṟār. kōliṉ varutalē ceyaṟpāṟ ṟeṉpataṟku ētu
   kūṟuvār, iṭaiyilē nikaḻnta nikaḻcciyai yeṭuttōtaluṟṟu, "kolkaḷiṟṟu
   yāṉai yerut tam pulleṉa vilkulai yaṟuttu" eṉṟār. aṟuppa veṉpatu
   aṟutteṉa niṉṟatu. kaṇṭum eṉa orucol varuvikka."yāṉai yeruttam poliyak
   kuṭainiḻaṟ kīḻc, cēṉait talaivarāy" (nālaṭi 3) vantārātaliṉ, vanta
   avarai veṉṟu mēmpaṭṭa ceytiyai, "yāṉai yeruttam pulleṉa vilkulai
   yaṟuttu" eṉṟār. kōliṉ vārāta vēntar pōr kuṟittu vantamai tōṉṟa"vel pōr
   vēntar" eṉṟār. iṉip paḻaiyavuraikārar"yāṉai yeruttam pulleṉa vilkulai
   yaṟuttuk kōliṉ vārā vēnta reṉṟatu, muṉpu niṉ vaḻi yoḻukātu oḻintirunta
   vaḻip piṉapu tām kaḷattu niṉpōr valikaṇṭu iṉi niṉ vaḻi yoḻukutumeṉac
   collit tām ēṟiya yāṉai yeruttam pulleṉa villiṉ nāṇiyai yaṟuttu niṉ
   ceṅkōlvaḻi yoḻukāta vēntareṉṟavā" ṟeṉpar
   pakai vēntarai"vel pōr vēntar" eṉṟār. itaṟku muṉpellām avar ceyta
   pōraṉaittiṉum veṉṟiyē yeyti vantamaiyum, accerukkē paṟṟukkōṭāka ippōtum
   vantāreṉpatum uṇarttutaṟku. ippōril tōṟṟamaiyāl avaratu muracum kaḷattē
   yoḻinta teṉṟaṟku, "avar muracukaṇ kiḻittu" eṉṟār.
   itukāṟum tām peṟṟuppōnta veṉṟiyāl tammaiyē viyantu tam paṭṭattiyāṉaimēl
   vantavar.* pōriṟpaṭṭu vīḻntamaiyiṉ, avaratu avyāṉaiyaippaṟṟi aḵtu
   āṟṟātu kataṟip pulampip piḷiṟa ataṉ kōṭṭiṉai yaṟuttu ataṉāla
   palikkaṭṭil ceytukoṇṭa ceytiyai "aracuvā vaḻaippak kōṭaṟut tiyaṟṟiya
   kaṭṭil" eṉṟum koṟṟa vaikkup pali yiṭuvatu kuṟittu viḻuppuṇpaṭṭa vīrar
   ataṉmītiruntu tam mārpiṟ puṇṉoloḻukum kuruti teḷittu maṭai koṭuppa,
   teyvamum ataṉai virumpi yēṟpatu kuṟittu ak kaṭṭilai"aṇaṅkuṭai marapiṟ
   kaṭṭil" eṉṟum ataṉmītiruntu kuruti viraviya maṭai koṭuppiṉaṉṟi yēlāta
   teyvam añcattakum muṟaimai yuṭaittātal paṟṟi, "añcuvaru marapiṟ
   kaṭavuḷ" eṉṟum kūṟiṉār.
   tumpai cūṭip porumvīrar pakaivarai yeṟikaiyil tāmum mukattiṉum mārpiṉum
   puṇpaṭṭu meytaḷara ōycca leytiyapōtum tam mārpiṟ puṇṇiṉ kurutiyaic
   ciṟitum tayaṅkātu aḷḷi maṭaiyiṉ puṟattē teṟippavātaliṉ, "tumpai cāṉṟa
   mey tayaṅkuyakkattu niṟampaṭu kuruti" yeṉṟār. paḻaiyavuraikārar, "
   tumpai cāṉṟa mey tayaṅkuyakkattu niṟampaṭu kuruti yeṉṟatu, vīraruṭaiya,
   tumpai cūṭiyataṟkēṟpa niṉṟu porutalāṟṟalaiyuṭaiya uṭalāṉatu acaiyumpaṭi
   vanta ōyviṉaiyuṭaiya niṟaṅkaḷait tiṟantuviṭṭa kuruti yeṉṟavā"ṟeṉṟum,
   "allāta iṭaṅḷiṟ kuruti koḷḷāmaiyiṉ niṟaṅkaḷait tiṟakka āṇṭuṇṭāṉa kuruti
   yeṉpatāyiṟ"ṟeṉṟum, "ic ciṟappāṉē itaṟku niṟampaṭu kuruti yeṉṟu
   peyarāyiṟ" ṟeṉṟum kūṟuvar
   ivvāṟē, "kōṭaṟut tiyaṟṟiya kaṭṭiṉ mēliruntu niṟampaṭu kuruti puṟampaṭi
   ṉallatu maṭai yetirkoḷḷāk; kaṭavuḷ eṉakkūṭṭi, avvāṟu ceytatoru kaṭṭil
   koṭuvantiṭ ṭataṉmēliruntu avvāṟu koṭuppatoru paliyuṇṭāyiṉallatu
   palikoḷḷāk kaṭavuḷeṉa vuraikka" eṉṟum"kaṭṭilmēliruntallatu kuruti
   puṟampaṭi ṉallatu eṉa alla teṉpataṉai iraṇṭiṭattum kūṭṭikkoḷka" eṉṟum
   kūṟuvar.
   "niṟampaṭu kuruti puṟampaṭi ṉallatu maṭai yetirkoḷḷāk kaṭavuḷ" eṉavē,
   koṟṟavai yeṉpatu peṟṟām. ayirai, cirāp paḷḷikku mēṟkil kāviriyiṉ
   teṟkiluḷḷatoru kuṉṟu; itaṉai ikkālattār aivarmalai yeṉṟu vaḻaṅkupa.
   paḻaiyavuraikārar, "akkoṟṟavai yuṟaiva torumalai" yeṉpar. ayirai malai
   niṉṟu nilai peṟuvatupōla niṉ pukaḻkaḷum nilaipeṟṟu viḷaṅkuka eṉpār,
   "ayiraiyiṉ nilaiik kēṭilavāka niṉ pukaḻē" yeṉṟār.
   itukāṟum kūṟiyavāṟṟāl, nāviṉaiyum āṇmaiyiṉaiyum mārpiṉaiya muṭaiyōy
   peruma, ceruvattu uyir pōṟṟalai; iravalar naṭuvaṇ koṭai pōṟṟalai;
   periyōrp pēṇic ciṟiyōrai yaḷitti; niṉvayiṉ nallicai kaṉaviṉum piṟar
   nacai yaṟiyā; aṉaiya aḷapparuṅ kuraiyai; ataṉāl niṉ pukaḻkaḷ ayiraiyiṉ
   nilaiik kēṭilavāka eṉpatām. iṉip paḻaiyavuraikārar"kōtai mārpa,
   ceruvattu uyir pōṟṟalai; iravalar naṭuvaṇ koṭai pōṟṟalai; periyōraip
   pēṇic ciṟiyōrai yaḷitti; aṉaiya niṉ kuṇaṅkaḷ aḷappariyai; nī avvā
   ṟoḻukutalāl, pirinta niṉvayiṉ nallicai iṉik kaṉavilum piṟar naccuta
   laṟiyā; avvāṟu aṟiyāmaiyiṉ, peruma, niṉ pukaḻ nilaii niṉṉiṭattuk
   kēṭilavāka eṉa māṟikkūṭṭi viṉaimuṭivu ceyka" eṉṟum, "aṉaiya vaḷapparuṅ
   kuraiyai yeṉṟatu ciṟiyōrai yaḷitti eṉṟataṉ piṉṉē niṟka
   vēṇṭutaliṉmāṟāyiṟ" ṟeṉṟum kūṟuvar.
   itaṉāṟ colliyatu avaṉ pala kuṇaṅkaḷum oruṅku pukaḻntu
   vāḻttiya-vāṟāyiṟṟu.

                    8.10. paṇṇuṭai yeṟuḻttōḷ

   80
   vāṉ maruppiṟ kaḷiṟṟiyāṉai
       māmalaiyiṟ kaṇaṅkoṇṭavar
       eṭutteṟinta viṟaṉmuracam
       kārmaḻaiyiṟ kaṭitumuḻaṅkac
       cāntupularnta viyaṉmārpiṟ         5
       ṟoṭicuṭarvarum valimuṉkaip
       puṇṇuṭai yeṟuḻttōṭ puṭaiyalaṅkaḻaṟkāṟ
       piṟakkaṭi yotuṅkāp pūṭkai yoḷvāḷ
       oṭivi ṟevva retirniṉ ṟuraii
       iṭuka tiṟaiyē puravetirn tōṟkeṉa         10
       ampuṭai valatta ruyarntōr parava
       aṉaiyai yākaṉ māṟē pakaivar
       kālkiḷarn taṉṉa kataḻparip puravik
       kaṭumpari neṭuntēr mīmicai nuṭaṅkukoṭi
       pilavarait tōṉṟal yāvatu ciṉappōr         15
       nilavarai niṟīiya nallicait
       tolaiyāk kaṟpaniṉ ṟemmuṉai yāṉē

   tuṟai: vañcittuṟaip pāṭāṇpāṭṭu
   vaṇṇam: oḻuku vaṇṇam
   tūkku: centūkkum vañcittūkkum
   peyar: puṇṇuṭai yeṟuḻttōḷ.
   1-4 vāṉ maruppiṉ........muḻaṅka
   urai: vāl maruppiṉ kaḷiṟṟuyāṉai māmalaiyiṉ kaṇaṅ koṇṭu - veḷḷiya
   maruppiṉaiyuṭaiya pōrkkaḷiṟukaḷ periya malaipōla kūṭit tokku niṟpa;
   avar eṭutteṟinta viṟal muracam - pakaivar mēṟkoṇṭu muḻakkiya veṟṟi
   muracamāṉatu; kār maḻaiyiṉ kaṭitu muḻaṅka - kār kālattu mukilpōla mikku
   muḻaṅkavum; pōrkkuriya āṇmai nalam ciṟantu niṟkum yāṉekaḷaik"kaḷiṟṟi
   yāṉai" yeṉṟār. malaiyiṉ, iṉ oppup poruṭṭu. avar eṉpatu "oṭivil tevvar"
   (9) eṉṟataṉaic cuṭṭi niṟṟaliṉ, cuṭṭu; ceyyuḷātaliṉ muṟpaṭa vantatu.
   koḷḷa veṉpatu koṇṭeṉat tirintu niṉṟatu. paḻaiyavuraikārarum, "kaṇaṅ
   koḷḷa veṉat tirikka" eṉṟum, "ava reṉṟatu pakaivarai" yeṉṟum kūṟuvar.
   eṭut teṟital, tam muṟṟukai tōṉṟa mēṟkoṇṭu muḻakkutal. veṟṟi kuṟittu
   muḻakkum muracātaliṉ, viṟal muracam" eṉṟār; pali peṟum ciṟappuppaṟṟi
   iṅṅaṉam kūṟiṉā reṉiṉumām. mikutip poruṭṭāya kaṭiyeṉṉu muriccol kaṭitu
   eṉat tirintu niṉṟatu.
   5-9 cāntu . . . . niṉṟu
   urai: cāntu pularnta viyaṉ mārpiṉ pūciya cāntu pularnta akaṉṟa
   mārpiṉaiyum; toṭi cuṭarvarum valimuṉkai- toṭi yaṇintamaiyāl ataṉ
   oḷitikaḻum valiporuntiya muṉkaiyiṉaiyum; puṇṇuṭai eṟuḻttōḷ - āṟāta
   viḻup puṇṇaiyuṭaiya valiya tōḷiṉaiyum; puṭaiyalaṅ; kaḻaṟkāl - at
   tōḷiṭattē yaṇinta mālaiyoṭu vīrakaṇṭai yaṇinta kāliṉaiyum; piṟakkaṭi
   yotuṅkāp pūṭkai - muṉvaittakālaip piṉ vaiyāta mēṟkōḷiṉaiyum; oḷvāḷ -
   oḷḷiya vāṭpaṭaiyiṉaiyumuṭaiya; oṭivil tevvar etirniṉṟu - vaṇaṅkāta
   pakaivar muṉṉē añcātu niṉṟu; e-ṟu.
   āṭavarkku akaṉṟa mārpu ciṟapput taruvatākaliṉ, ataṉai vitantu "cāntu
   pularnta viyaṉ mārpiṉ" eṉṟār. toṭi, tōḷvaḷai. ivarkaḷ ēntiyaṭum
   vāṭpaṭaiyai"oḷvāḷ eṉac ciṟappittaliṉ, ataṟkēṟpa, "valimuṉ kai" yeṉṟār.
   pala pōrkaḷaic ceytu veṉṟi mēmpaṭṭō reṉṟaṟku, avar uṟṟa puṇṇai vitantu,
   "puṇṇuṭai yeṟuḻttōḷ" eṉṟum, puṇṇuṭaittākiyum vali kuṟaintatiṉ ṟeṉṟaṟkup
   puṇṇuṭaittō ḷeṉṉātu, "eṟuḻttō" ḷeṉṟum kūṟiṉār. iṉip paḻaiyavuraikārar,
   puṇṇuṭai yeṟuḻttō ḷeṉṟatu eppoḻutum porutu puṇṇāṟāta valiya tōḷeṉṟavā"
   ṟeṉṟum, "ic ciṟappāṉē itaṟkup puṇṇuṭai yeṟuḻttōḷ eṉṟu peyarāyiṟ" ṟeṉṟum
   kūṟuvar.
   puṭaiyalaṅkaḻal, ummaittokai. puṭaiyal mālai."īkaiyaṅ kaḻaṟkāl
   irumpaṉampuṭaiyal" (puṟam.99) eṉṟum, "māyirum puṭaiyal mākkaḻal
   puṉaintu (patiṟ. 37) eṉṟum, "irumpaṉam puṭaiyalīkai vāṉkaḻal"
   (patiṟ.42) eṉṟum, "irumpaṉam puṭaiyaloṭu vāṉkaḻal civappa" (patiṟ. 57)
   eṉṟum"puṭaiyalaṅkaḻaṟkāl pulli" (akam. 295) eṉṟum ippuṭaiyal kaḻaloṭu
   piṇaittē kūṟappaṭutaliṉ, itu kaḻalaṇiyum vīrar aṭaiyāḷamākac cūṭum
   pūmālaiyātal tuṇiyappaṭum. irumpaṉam puṭaiyaleṉat terittu
   moḻivatum"puṭaiyal" eṉa vāḷātu kūṟuvatum ikkaruttai valiyuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa.
   īṇṭut tevvar cūṭiya puṭaiyalait terittu moḻiyāmaiyiṉ, paṉampuṭaiyaleṉak
   kōṭal poruntāmai yaṟika.
   aṭipiṟakkiṭāta mēṟkōḷ vīrarkku iṉṟiyamai yāmaiyiṉ, "piṟakkaṭiyotuṅkāp
   pūṭkai" eṉṟār."aṭiyotuṅkip piṟpeyarāp paṭaiyōr" (maturai. 37-8) eṉṟu
   piṟarum kūṟupa. oṭital, maṟaṅkuṉṟip pakaivarkku vaṇaṅkutal;
   aṟaipōtalumām.
   ittakaiya oṭivil tevvar viṟal muracam muḻaṅka muṟṟukai yiṭṭu niṟpavum,
   avarmuṉ ciṟitum accamilarāy niṉṟu vīṟu pēcum cēramāṉ vīrar
   maṟanilaiyai"etirniṉṟu" eṉpataṉāl tōṟṟuvikkiṉṟār.
   9-17 uraii.............temmuṉaiyāṉē
   urai: uyarntōr - niṉtāṉai vīrarākiya uyarntōrkaḷ; ampuṭai valattar -
   iṭak kaiyil villum valak kaiyil ampumuṭaiyarāy; uraii - at tevvar muṉṉē
   niṉṟu iru maruṅkum ulāvi; paravu etirtantōṟku tiṟai iṭuka - tiṟai
   celuttit taṉ kōṟkīḻp paṇintu niṟpārkkup pātukāppiṉai vaḻaṅkutaṟku
   ēṟaṭṭukkoṇṭu niṟkum eṅkaḷ peruñ cēralirumpoṟaikku nuṅkaḷ tiṟaiyiṉaic
   celuttuvīrāmiṉ; eṉap parava - eṉṟu niṉkoṭaiyum aḷiyum teṟalum piṟavum
   pārāṭṭik kūṟa; aṉaiyai yākaṉmāṟē - nīyum avar kūṟum nalamellām uṭaiyai
   yātaliṉālē; ciṉap pōr-ciṉaṅ koṇṭu ceyyum pōriṉaiyum; nilavarai niṟīiya
   nallicai-nila vulakattē niṟuvappaṭṭa nalla icaiyiṉaiyum; tolaiyāk
   kaṟpa- kēṭillāta kalviyiṉaiyumuṭaiyāy; niṉ temmuṉaiyāṉ-nī
   pakaikoṇṭāṟṟum pōr muṉaiyākiya; pula varai-taṅkaḷ nilavellaiyiṟṟāṉum;
   pakaivar-niṉ pakaivaratu; kāl kiḷarntaṉṉa kataḻ parip puravi-kāṟṟuk
   kiḷarntu ceṉṟāṟpōlum virainta celaviṉaiyuṭaiya kutiraikaḷ pūṭṭiya;
   kaṭumpari neṭuntēr mīmicai-kaṭuñ celaviṉaiyuṭaiya neṭiya tērmītu
   kaṭṭiya; nuṭaṅku koṭi tōṉṟutal yāvatu-acaikiṉṟa koṭi tōṉṟuvatu
   eṅṅaṉamākuma; iṉi aḵtu evvāṟṟāṉum tōṉṟātukāṇ e-ṟu
   pōrvīrarkku vēṇṭum uyarkuṇaṅka ḷellām oruṅkuṭaimai paṟṟi"uyarntōr"
   eṉṟār. pakai vīraruṭaiya mārpu mutaliyavaṟṟai vitantu kūṟiya āciriyar
   avarkaḷai uyarntō reṉṟatu, avarkaṭku ak kūṟiya ciṟappaṉaittum āra vāra
   māttiraiyē eṉa vaṟpuṟuttiyavāṟu. ampuṭai valattareṉavē, villuṭaimai
   tāṉē peṟappaṭumākaliṉ, atu kūṟārāyiṉār. iṉi, ampuṭai valattar eṉṟaṟku,
   ampiṉālākiya veṟṟiyaiyuṭaiya reṉṟumām. tevvar muṉ niṉṟu ivvuyarntōr
   cēraṉuṭaiya talaimaip paṇpukaḷaiyum āṇmai vaṇmaikaḷaiyum paravik
   kūṟaluṟṟōr, tam kūṟṟu pakaivar paṭaipparappu muṟṟum kēṭṭal vēṇṭi
   irumaruṅkum ulavic ceṉṟu uraittār eṉṟaṟku"uraii" yeṉṟum, paṭai tiraṇṭu
   pōr kuṟittu vantīrāyiṉum tiṟaiyiṭiṉ eṅkaḷ iṟaivaṉ nuṅkaḷaip poṟuttup
   puravu pūṇpaṉ eṉpār"iṭuka tiṟaiyē puravetirn tōṟkē" eṉṟārkaḷ eṉṟum
   kūṟiṉar. av vuyarntōr niṉṉaippaṟṟik kūṟiya aṉaittum meyyē yeṉpār,
   "aṉaiyai yākaṉmāṟē" yeṉṟār. māṟu, mūṉṟām vēṟṟumaip poruṭṭu.
   pakaivaratu kutiraiyiṉ tiṟam kūṟuvār, "kālkiḷarntaṉṉa kaṭum
   parippuravi" yeṉṟum avaṟṟaip pūṭṭum tērum ittakait teṉṟaṟkuk kaṭumpari
   neṭuntēr" eṉṟum"kālkiḷarntaṉṉa vēḻam" (muruku.) eṉṟum cāṉṟōr virainta
   naṭaikkuk kāṟṟai uvamam kūṟuvatu kāṇka. ciṟappummai vikārattāl tokkatu.
   niṉ nallicai nilavarai muḻutum paravi, nilaipeṟutaliṉ, pakaivaratu koṭi
   nuṭaṅkutaṟkum iṭaṉillai eṉpatu kūṟiyavāṟu. nallicai niṟuvutaṟkēṟṟa
   kalviyum mika vuṭaiyāy eṉpār"tolaiyāk kaṟpa" eṉṟār.
   iṉi paḻaiyavuraikārar, "puravetirntōṟ keṉṟatu koṭai yēṟṟirukkiṉṟa
   avaṉukkeṉṟavā" ṟeṉṟum, "koṭi tōṉṟal eṉṟataṉai eḻuvāyum payaṉilaiyumākak
   koḷka" eṉṟum"niṉ temmuṉaip pulavaraiyāṉ eṉa māṟik kūṭṭuka" eṉṟum
   kūṟuvar.
   itukāṟum kūṟiyatu, kaḷiṟṟiyāṉai kaṇaṅkoḷḷa, avar eṟinta muracam
   muḻaṅkavum, mārpiṉaiyum, muṉkaiyiṉaiyum, tōḷiṉaiyum, puṭaiyalaiyum,
   kaḻaṟkālaiyum, pūṭkaiyiṉaiyum, vāḷiṉaiyumuṭai oṭivil tevvar etirniṉṟu,
   uyarntōr, ampuṭaiya valattarāy uraii puravetirntōṟku iṭuka tiṟaiyē
   yeṉapparava, aṉaiyai yākaṉmāṟē ciṉappōrum nallicaiyum kaṟpiṉaiyu
   muṭaiyāy, niṉ temmuṉaiyākiya pulavaraiyil, pakaivar kataḻparip puravik
   kaṭuntēr mīmicai neṭuṅkoṭi tōṉṟal yāvatu eṉpatām. iṉip
   paḻaiyavuraikārar, "tolaiyāk kaṟpa, niṉ vīrarākiya uyarntōr niṉ
   tevvarākiya avaruṭaiya kaḷiṟṟi yāṉai malaiyiṟ kaṇaṅ koḷḷāniṟka, muracam
   kaṭitu muḻaṅkāniṟka, avaiyiṟṟai oṉṟum matiyātē niṉṉoṭu oṭivil
   tevvarākiya avar etir niṉṟu peyarā ippura vetirntōṉukkut tiṟaiyai
   yiṭuka veṉac colli niṉṉaip paravumpaṭi nī ataṟkēṟṟa taṉmaiyai
   yuṭaiyaiyāṉa paṭiyālē niṉ temmuṉaip pula vellaiyil niṉ pakaivar
   tērmicaik koṭi pōraik kuṟittut tōṉṟal yāvatu eṉakkūṭṭi viṉai muṭivu
   ceyka" eṉpar.
   itaṉāṟ colliyatu avaṉ koṭaicciṟappoṭu paṭuttu veṉṟic ciṟappuk
   kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu,
   "temmuṉaip pulavaraip pakaivar koṭi tōṉṟal yāvatu eṉa etirūṉṟuvāriṉmai
   tōṉṟak kūṟiya vataṉāl vañcit tuṟaip pāṭāṇāyiṟṟu.
   "muṉṉar āṟaṭiyum vañciyaṭiyāy vantamaiyāṉē vañcittūkku māyiṟṟu"
   iruvakait tūkkum viravivantatāyiṉum āciriya naṭaiyē peṟṟu iṉiya
   ōcaikoṇṭu varutaliṉ oḻuku vaṇṇamāyiṟṟu;"oḻuku vaṇṇam ōcaiyiṉoḻukum"
   (tol. cey. 224) eṉṟārākaliṉ.
   eṭṭām pattu mūlamum uraiyum muṟṟum.

                              oṉpatām pattu

   patikam
   kuṭṭuva ṉirumpoṟaikku maiyūr kiḻāṉ
   vēṇmā ḷantuvañ ceḷḷai yīṉṟamakaṉ
   verutaru tāṉaiyoṭu veytuṟac ceytuceṉ
   ṟiruperu vēntarum vicciyum vīḻa
   arumiḷaik kallakat tainteyi leṟintu 5
   potti yāṇṭa peruñ cōḻaṉaiyum
   vittai yāṇṭa viḷampaḻaiyaṉ māṟaṉaiyum
   vaitta vañciṉam vāyppa veṉṟu
   vañci mutūrt tantu piṟarkkutavi
   mantira marapiṟ ṟeyvam pēṇi 10
   meyyū ramaicciyaṉ maiyūr kiḻāṉaip
   puraiyaṟu kēḷvip purōcu mayakki
   aruntiṟaṉ marapiṟ peruñcatuk kamarnta
   ventiṟaṟ pūtarait tantiva ṇiṟīi
   āynta marapiṟ cānti vēṭṭu 15
   maṉṉuyir kātta maṟuvil ceṅkōl
   iṉṉicai muraci ṉiḷañcēra lirumpoṟaiyaip
   peruṅkuṉṟūr kiḻār pāṭiṉār pattuppāṭṭu.
   avaitām: niḻal viṭukaṭṭi, viṉainavil yāṉai, paḵṟōṟ ṟoḻuti,toḻiṉavil
   yāṉai, nāṭukāṇ neṭuvarai, ventiṟaṟ ṟaṭakkai, veṇṭalaic cempuṉal, kalkāl
   kavaṇai, tuvarāk kūntal, valikeḻu taṭakkai, ivai pāṭṭiṉ patikam. pāṭip
   peṟṟa paricil: maruḷillārkku maruḷak koṭukkaveṉṟu uvakaiyiṉ
   muppattīrāyiram kāṇaṅ koṭuttu, avar aṟiyāmai ūrum maṉaiyum vaḷamikap
   paṭaittu, ērum iṉpamum iyalvarap parappi, eṇṇaṟkākā aruṅkala
   veṟukkaiyoṭu paṉṉūṟāyiram pāṟpaṭa vakuttuk kāppu maṟam tāṉviṭṭāṉ ak kō.
   kuṭak kō iḷañcēra lirumpoṟai patiṉāṟāṇṭu vīṟṟiruntāṉ.
